{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_016MYHRo9Fvjrzg7H5Z3eDa7","content":"Launching skill: standards"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"c7182ca6-6dc3-42ea-b6ec-91513e16135f","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:26:35.513Z","tool_use_result":{"success":true,"commandName":"standards"}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/.claude/skills/standards\n\n# Ethereum Standards\n\nAll of these are deployed and in production use, not proposals: **ERC-8004** (agent identity + reputation, Ethereum mainnet 2026-01-29), **x402** (HTTP payments), **EIP-3009** (gasless USDC transfers, what makes x402 practical), **EIP-7702** (smart EOAs, live with Pectra 2025-05-07). When a design needs agent discovery, third-party-attested reputation, per-call billing without accounts, or contract abilities on an address a user already owns, name the standard instead of rebuilding it — an API-key tier, a reviews table you host, or a migration to a fresh smart account are all the wrong answer to a problem one of these already solves.\n\n## ERC-8004 — agent identity and reputation\n\nTwo registries, deployed at the same pair of addresses across 40+ chains — but **the testnet set is a different pair**, and the mainnet addresses have no code on Sepolia or Base Sepolia:\n\n| | mainnets | testnets |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| IdentityRegistry (ERC-721) | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` | `0x8004A818BFB912233c491871b3d84c89A494BD9e` |\n| ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` | `0x8004B663056A597Dffe9eCcC1965A193B7388713` |\n\n- `register()` mints an ERC-721. **The agentId is the tokenId the registry assigns** — never a UUID, hash, or slug you pick. Callers address the agent chain-scoped: `agentRegistry: eip155:8453:0x8004A169…` (Base) plus that `agentId`.\n- The `agentURI` (the ERC-721 tokenURI) MUST resolve to the registration file — `ipfs://`, `https://`, or a base64 `data:` URI. It carries `type: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\"`, `name`, `description`, `image`, a `services` array (A2A card, MCP, ENS, DID, email…), `x402Support`, `active`, a `registrations` array of `{agentId, agentRegistry}`, and optional `supportedTrust`.\n- **Bind the endpoint domain, the step that gets skipped.** If the domain serving the advertised endpoints is not the domain serving the agentURI file, publish `https://{endpoint-domain}/.well-known/agent-registration.json` with at least a `registrations` entry whose `agentRegistry` and `agentId` match the onchain agent. An A2A `agent-card.json` is a different document and does not do this. If the same domain already serves the agentURI file, control is proven there — say which of the two you are relying on rather than leaving the endpoints unbound.\n- Reputation is **client-attested**: the caller who paid posts `giveFeedback(agentId, value, valueDecimals, tag1, tag2, endpoint, feedbackURI, feedbackHash)`; the registry rejects feedback from the agent's owner and operators, so you cannot rate yourself. Values are fixed point — 99.77% uptime is `value=9977, valueDecimals=2`; 87/100 quality is `value=87, valueDecimals=0`. Read with `getSummary(agentId, clientAddresses, tag1, tag2)` and filter to clients you trust; unfiltered totals are Sybil bait.\n- The spec also defines a Validation Registry (re-execution, zkML, TEE attestation, 0–100 validator scores). It is not part of the deployed pair above — check before designing on it.\n- The contracts are live; the EIP is still **Draft**, so treat anything you have not verified onchain as movable. Registries are ERC-1967 proxies: read the implementation slot if a signature surprises you.\n\n## x402 — pay per HTTP call, no accounts\n\n`GET` → `402` + `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` → client signs → retry with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` → server verifies and settles → `200` + `PAYMENT-RESPONSE`. A facilitator (Coinbase runs a public one, anyone can run their own) performs verify/settle so the resource server never runs a node or holds gas.\n\nAmounts go on the wire in **token base units**: $0.35 USDC is `350000` (USDC has 6 decimals) — not `0.35`, not an 18-decimal figure. Native USDC on Base is `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`; `0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48` is mainnet USDC and `0xd9aAEc86B65D86f6A7B5B1b0c42FFA531710b6CA` is the bridged USDbC — both are the wrong contract for a Base x402 route.\n\nThe maintained TypeScript line is the scoped one: `@x402/core`, `@x402/evm`, `@x402/fetch`, `@x402/express`, with `@coinbase/x402` as the facilitator config beside it. Unscoped `x402`, `x402-fetch`, `x402-express` are frozen at 1.2.0; Go lives at `github.com/x402-foundation/x402/go/v2`. **Do not write the integration from a remembered snippet** — `x402Fetch` and `createWallet` do not exist in the scoped packages, and a one-argument `paymentMiddleware(config)` is the v1 shape. Read the installed types: on 2.x the Express middleware takes routes plus a resource server, and the fetch wrapper takes an x402 client, not a bare account.\n\n**A caller with USDC and no ETH can still pay**, because USDC implements **EIP-3009**: the client signs a `transferWithAuthorization` offchain and the server or facilitator submits it and pays the gas. That is the mechanism to name — not ERC-4337 bundlers, not an unnamed \"relayer\", not ERC-2612 permit followed by `transferFrom`.\n\n## EIP-7702 — contract code on an EOA the user already has\n\nLive on Ethereum mainnet since Pectra, **2025-05-07**. The EOA signs an authorization **with its own key** (a type `0x04` transaction) that writes a delegation designator, `0xef0100 || implementation address`, into the account. Calls to that same address then execute the implementation's code — an approve+swap batched atomically, sponsored gas, session-key auth — with the address, key, ENS name and history untouched. Deploying a fresh ERC-4337 wallet instead puts the user at a **new address**, which is what to reject whenever the existing address has to survive.\n\nThe delegation **persists**. It stays in place until a later authorization replaces it, or clears it by delegating to the zero address. It is not single-transaction-only and does not auto-revert when the batched call finishes.\n\n## Status at a glance\n\n| Standard | What | Status |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| ERC-8004 | Agent identity + reputation | Live — Ethereum mainnet 2026-01-29 (EIP still Draft) |\n| x402 | HTTP 402 payments | Live — scoped SDKs on 2.x |\n| EIP-3009 | Gasless token transfers | Live — USDC implements it |\n| EIP-7702 | Smart EOAs | Live — Pectra, 2025-05-07 |\n| ERC-4337 | Account abstraction (new account, new address) | Live, widely deployed |\n| ERC-2612 / ERC-4626 / ERC-6551 | Permit / tokenized vaults / token-bound accounts | Live |\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"44c0cae9-5660-4991-a136-b3ff49d2c92c","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:26:35.510Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016MvrQrHzemc1tQBW5kxzMa","type":"tool_result","content":"Build the service side of `forecast.example.com`: it sells weather forecasts to\nautonomous agents for $0.35 a call, settled in USDC on Base. It has to work\nunder these constraints:\n\n- A client agent that has never contacted you before must be able to discover\n  the service and judge whether it is worth trusting, without a human reading\n  your docs and without relying on a catalog you own or operate.\n- Billing is per call. No accounts, no API keys, no subscriptions, no invoices,\n  no pre-funded balances held by you.\n- The calling agent holds USDC but no ETH, and that must not stop it paying.\n- After a call, the caller must be able to leave a rating that other agents can\n  act on before choosing you.\n- No step in the flow may require a human.\n\nWrite design.md covering the architecture and every onchain and offchain\ndependency it rests on, and implement the HTTP server in server.ts. Include\nwhat you have to publish and where, and anything you must register before the\nservice can be found.\n---\n2.23.0","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"50d44d30-d636-462c-819a-76735a07f874","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:26:43.161Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"Build the service side of `forecast.example.com`: it sells weather forecasts to\nautonomous agents for $0.35 a call, settled in USDC on Base. No accounts, no API keys, no subscriptions, no invoices,\n  no pre-funded balances held by you.\n- The calling agent holds USDC but no ETH, and that must not stop it paying.\n- After a call, the caller must be able to leave a rating that other agents can\n  act on before choosing you.\n- No step in the flow may require a human.\n\nWrite design.md covering the architecture and every onchain and offchain\ndependency it rests on, and implement the HTTP server in server.ts. getBody(): unknown;\n}\n\n/**\n * Set settlement overrides on the response for partial settlement.\n * The middleware will extract these before settlement and strip the header from the client app.use(paymentMiddlewareFromHTTPServer(httpServer));\n * ```\n */\ndeclare function paymentMiddlewareFromHTTPServer(httpServer: x402HTTPResourceServer, paywallConfig?: PaywallConfig, paywall?: PaywallProvider, syncFacilitatorOnStart?: boolean): (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => Promise<void>;\n/**\n * Express payment middleware for x402 protocol (direct server paymentMiddleware(routes: RoutesConfig, server: x402ResourceServer, paywallConfig?: PaywallConfig, paywall?: PaywallProvider, syncFacilitatorOnStart?: boolean): (req: Request, res: Response, next: {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XhrYLMqGCKkh2wYN9GGnBh","type":"tool_result","content":"     */\n    private toJsonSafe;\n}\n\n/**\n * Which settle invocation is running for a payment.\n *\n * - `before-handler` — settle before the resource handler (e.g. escrow deposit)\n * - `after-handler` — settle after the resource handler (authorization charge, escrow charge)\n * - `cancel` — refund/close settle from verified-payment cancellation\n *\n * Settle lifecycle hooks (`beforeSettle`, `afterSettle`, `onSettleFailure`,\n * `enrichSettlementPayload`, `enrichSettlementResponse`) fire once per settle.\n * Multi-settle flows (`escrow`) therefore invoke them more than once; branch on\n * this field when a hook has side effects that must not double-run.\n */\ntype SettlePhase = \"before-handler\" | \"after-handler\" | \"cancel\";\n/**\n * Configuration for a protected resource\n * Only contains payment-specific configuration, not resource metadata\n */\ninterface ResourceConfig {\n    scheme: string;\n    /**\n     * Payment recipient. Use a **vacant** value (`\"\"` or whitespace-only) when an extension must\n     * fill `payTo` during `enrichPaymentRequiredResponse`; non-vacant values are **immutable** there\n     * so extensions cannot redirect funds to an arbitrary address.\n     */\n    payTo: string;\n    price: Price;\n    network: Network;\n    maxTimeoutSeconds?: number;\n    extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n/**\n * Context for `enrichPaymentRequiredResponse`. Extensions may merge extension payload via the\n * return value. In-place edits to `paymentRequiredResponse.accepts` are **allowlisted** only\n * (see {@link assertAcceptsAllowlistedAfterExtensionEnrich}): `scheme`, `network`, and\n * `maxTimeoutSeconds` are immutable; `payTo`, `amount`, and `asset` may change only when the\n * baseline value was vacant; `extra` may add keys but must not change or remove baseline keys.\n * `extra.paymentFlow` and `extra.assetTransferMethod` are protocol-reserved and immutable\n * during enrichment (enrichment must not add or rewrite them).\n */\ninterface PaymentRequiredContext {\n    requirements: PaymentRequirements[];\n    resourceInfo: ResourceInfo;\n    error?: string;\n    paymentRequiredResponse: PaymentRequired;\n    transportContext?: unknown;\n}\n/**\n * Verify / settle lifecycle hook context: treat as **read-only** for core protocol fields.\n * Control flow uses **abort** / **recover** return values only, not in-place mutation.\n */\ninterface VerifyContext {\n    paymentPayload: DeepReadonly<PaymentPayload>;\n    requirements: DeepReadonly<PaymentRequirements>;\n    declaredExtensions: DeepReadonly<Record<string, unknown>>;\n    transportContext?: unknown;\n}\ninterface VerifyResultContext extends VerifyContext {\n    result: DeepReadonly<VerifyResponse>;\n}\n/**\n * Optional acknowledgement body returned to the caller when an `AfterVerifyHook`\n * requests that the resource handler be skipped for a self-contained operation\n * (e.g. cooperative refund). Travels in-process only — never on the facilitator wire.\n */\ninterface SkipHandlerDirective {\n    contentType?: string;\n    body?: unknown;\n/**\n * Dynamic function to generate a custom response for settlement failures.\n * Receives the HTTP request context and settle failure result, returns the content type and body.\n */\ntype SettlementFailedResponseBody = (context: HTTPRequestContext, settleResult: Omit<ProcessSettleFailureResponse, \"response\">) => HTTPResponseBody | Promise<HTTPResponseBody>;\n/**\n * A single payment option for a route\n * Represents one way a client can pay for access to the resource\n */\ninterface PaymentOption {\n    scheme: string;\n    payTo: string | DynamicPayTo;\n    price: Price | DynamicPrice;\n    network: Network;\n    maxTimeoutSeconds?: number;\n    extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n/**\n * Route configuration for HTTP endpoints\n *\n * The 'accepts' field defines payment options for the route.\n * Can be a single PaymentOption or an array of PaymentOptions for multiple payment methods.\n */\ninterface RouteConfig {\n    accepts: PaymentOption | PaymentOption[];\n    resource?: string;\n    description?: string;\n    mimeType?: string;\n    serviceName?: string;\n    tags?: string[];\n    iconUrl?: string;\n    customPaywallHtml?: string;\n    /**\n     * Optional callback to generate a custom response for unpaid API requests.\n     * This allows servers to return preview data, error messages, or other content\n     * when a request lacks payment.\n     *\n     * For browser requests (Accept: text/html), the paywall HTML takes precedence.\n     * This callback is only used for API clients.\n     *\n     * If not provided, defaults to { contentType: 'application/json', body: {} }.\n     *\n     * @param context - The HTTP request context\n     * @returns An object containing both contentType and body for the 402 response\n     */\n    unpaidResponseBody?: UnpaidResponseBody;\n    /**\n     * Optional callback to generate a custom response for settlement failures.\n     * If not provided, defaults to { contentType: 'application/json', body: {} }.\n     *\n     * @param context - The HTTP request context\n     * @param settleResult - The settlement failure result\n     * @returns An object containing both contentType and body for the 402 response\n     */\n    settlementFailedResponseBody?: SettlementFailedResponseBody;\n    extensions?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n/**\n * Routes configuration - maps path patterns to route configs\n */\ntype RoutesConfig = Record<string, RouteConfig> | RouteConfig;\n/**\n * Check if any routes in the configuration declare bazaar extensions.\n *\n * @param routes - Route configuration\n * @returns True if any route has extensions.bazaar defined\n */\ndeclare function checkIfBazaarNeeded(routes: RoutesConfig): boolean;\n/**\n * Hook that runs on every request to a protected route, before payment processing.\n * Can grant access without payment, deny the request, or continue to payment flow.\n *\n * @returns\n * - `void` - Continue to payment processing (default behavior)\n * - `{ grantAccess: true }` - Grant access without requiring payment\n * - `{ abort: true; reason: string }` - Deny the request (returns 403)\n */\ntype ProtectedRequestHook = (context: HTTPRequestContext, routeConfig: RouteConfig) => Promise<void | {\n    grantAccess: true;\n} | {\n    abort: true;\n     * Return value merges into `settleResult.extensions[key]`. Facilitator fields (`success`,\n     * `transaction`, `network`, etc.) must not be changed; only `extensions` is merged from the hook.\n     */\n    enrichSettlementResponse?: (declaration: unknown, context: SettleResultContext) => Promise<unknown>;\n    /** Installed on `registerExtension`; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016n9kLemZwGgrGCJZd37mTS","type":"tool_result","content":"=== exact/server ===\nimport { SchemeNetworkServer, MoneyParser, Network, Price, AssetAmount, PaymentRequirements } from '@x402/core/types';\nimport { A as AssetTransferMethod } from '../../types-BBC1mC8d.js';\nimport { x402ResourceServer } from '@x402/core/server';\n\n/**\n * EVM server implementation for the Exact payment scheme.\n */\ndeclare class ExactEvmScheme implements SchemeNetworkServer {\n    readonly scheme = \"exact\";\n    readonly defaultAssetTransferMethod: AssetTransferMethod;\n    readonly paymentFlows: {\n        readonly eip3009: {\n            readonly supported: readonly [\"authorization\"];\n            readonly default: \"authorization\";\n        };\n        readonly permit2: {\n            readonly supported: readonly [\"authorization\"];\n            readonly default: \"authorization\";\n        };\n    };\n    private moneyParsers;\n    /**\n     * Register a custom money parser in the parser chain.\n     * Multiple parsers can be registered - they will be tried in registration order.\n     * Each parser receives a decimal string (e.g., \"1.50\" for $1.50).\n     * If a parser returns null, the next parser in the chain will be tried.\n     * The default parser is always the final fallback.\n     *\n     * @param parser - Custom function to convert amount to AssetAmount (or null to skip)\n     * @returns The server instance for chaining\n     *\n     * @example\n     * evmServer.registerMoneyParser(async (amount, network) => {\n     *   // Custom conversion logic\n     *   if (Number(amount) > 100) {\n     *     // Use different token for large amounts\n     *     return { amount: convertToTokenAmount(String(amount), 18), asset: \"0xCustomToken\" };\n     *   }\n     *   return null; // Use next parser\n     * });\n     */\n    registerMoneyParser(parser: MoneyParser): ExactEvmScheme;\n    /**\n     * Decimals for a known default asset, or undefined.\n     *\n     * @param asset - Asset address or symbol\n     * @param network - Target network\n     * @returns Decimals when the asset is a known default; otherwise undefined\n     */\n    getAssetDecimals(asset: string, network: Network): number | undefined;\n    /**\n     * Parses a price into an asset amount.\n     * If price is already an AssetAmount, returns it directly.\n     * If price is Money (string | number), parses to decimal and tries custom parsers.\n     * Falls back to default conversion if all custom parsers return null.\n     *\n     * @param price - The price to parse\n     * @param network - The network to use\n     * @returns Promise that resolves to the parsed asset amount\n     */\n    parsePrice(price: Price, network: Network): Promise<AssetAmount>;\n    /**\n     * Build payment requirements for this scheme/network combination\n     *\n     * @param paymentRequirements - The base payment requirements\n     * @param supportedKind - The supported kind from facilitator (unused)\n     * @param supportedKind.x402Version - The x402 version\n     * @param supportedKind.scheme - The logical payment scheme\n     * @param supportedKind.network - The network identifier in CAIP-2 format\n     * @param supportedKind.extra - Optional extra metadata regarding scheme/network implementation details\n     * @param extensionKeys - Extension keys supported by the facilitator (unused)\n     * @returns Payment requirements ready to be sent to clients\n     */\n    enhancePaymentRequirements(paymentRequirements: PaymentRequirements, supportedKind: {\n        x402Version: number;\n        scheme: string;\n        network: Network;\n        extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\n    }, extensionKeys: string[]): Promise<PaymentRequirements>;\n    /**\n     * Converts a numeric dollar amount to an AssetAmount using the default token for the network.\n     *\n     * @param amount - The decimal amount as a string\n     * @param network - The target network\n     * @param symbol - Optional ticker from a suffixed price\n     * @returns The converted asset amount with token metadata\n     */\n    private defaultMoneyConversion;\n}\n\n/**\n * Configuration options for registering EVM schemes to an x402ResourceServer\n */\ninterface EvmResourceServerConfig {\n    /**\n     * Optional specific networks to register\n     * If not provided, registers wildcard support (eip155:*)\n     */\n    networks?: Network[];\n}\n/**\n * Registers EVM exact payment schemes to an x402ResourceServer instance.\n *\n * This function registers:\n * - V2: eip155:* wildcard scheme with ExactEvmScheme (or specific networks if provided)\n *\n * @param server - The x402ResourceServer instance to register schemes to\n * @param config - Configuration for EVM resource server registration\n * @returns The server instance for chaining\n *\n * @example\n * ```typescript\n * import { registerExactEvmScheme } from \"@x402/evm/exact/server/register\";\n * import { x402ResourceServer } from \"@x402/core/server\";\n *\n * const server = new x402ResourceServer(facilitatorClient);\n * registerExactEvmScheme(server, {});\n * ```\n */\ndeclare function registerExactEvmScheme(server: x402ResourceServer, config?: EvmResourceServerConfig): x402ResourceServer;\n\nexport { type EvmResourceServerConfig, ExactEvmScheme, registerExactEvmScheme };\n\n=== coinbase/x402 ===\n[ '.' ]\nimport { FacilitatorConfig } from '@x402/core/http';\n\n/**\n * Creates an authorization header for a request to the Coinbase API.\n *\n * @param apiKeyId - The api key ID\n * @param apiKeySecret - The api key secret\n * @param requestMethod - The method for the request (e.g. 'POST')\n * @param requestHost - The host for the request (e.g. 'https://x402.org/facilitator')\n * @param requestPath - The path for the request (e.g. '/verify')\n * @returns The authorization header string\n */\ndeclare function createAuthHeader(apiKeyId: string, apiKeySecret: string, requestMethod: string, requestHost: string, requestPath: string): Promise<string>;\n/**\n * Creates a correlation header for a request to the Coinbase API.\n *\n * @returns The correlation header string\n */\ndeclare function createCorrelationHeader(): string;\n/**\n * Creates a CDP auth header for the facilitator service\n *\n * @param apiKeyId - The CDP API key ID\n * @param apiKeySecret - The CDP API key secret\n * @returns A function that returns the auth headers\n */\ndeclare function createCdpAuthHeaders(apiKeyId?: string, apiKeySecret?: string): FacilitatorConfig[\"createAuthHeaders\"];\n/**\n * Creates a facilitator config for the Coinbase X402 facilitator\n *\n * @param apiKeyId - The CDP API key ID\n * @param apiKeySecret - The CDP API key secret\n * @returns A facilitator config\n */\ndeclare function createFacilitatorConfig(apiKeyId?: string, apiKeySecret?: string): FacilitatorConfig;\ndeclare const facilitator: FacilitatorConfig;\n\nexport { createAuthHeader, createCdpAuthHeaders, createCorrelationHeader, createFacilitatorConfig, facilitator };","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"9fc4dafb-7e4d-4c60-9947-449621268d7b","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:27:45.587Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"=== exact/server ===\nimport { SchemeNetworkServer, MoneyParser, Network, Price, AssetAmount, PaymentRequirements } from '@x402/core/types';\nimport { A as AssetTransferMethod } from '../../types-BBC1mC8d.js';\nimport { x402ResourceServer } from '@x402/core/server';\n\n/**\n * EVM server implementation for the Exact payment scheme.\n */\ndeclare class ExactEvmScheme implements SchemeNetworkServer {\n    readonly scheme = \"exact\";\n    readonly defaultAssetTransferMethod: AssetTransferMethod;\n    readonly paymentFlows: {\n        readonly eip3009: {\n            readonly supported: readonly [\"authorization\"];\n            readonly default: \"authorization\";\n        };\n        readonly permit2: {\n            readonly supported: readonly [\"authorization\"];\n            readonly default: \"authorization\";\n        };\n    };\n    private moneyParsers;\n    /**\n     * Register a custom money parser in the parser chain.\n     * Multiple parsers can be registered - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EUqP2Jf8oYLCTKYL6FxoRt","type":"tool_result","content":"/**\n * Asset transfer methods for the exact EVM scheme.\n * - eip3009: Uses transferWithAuthorization (USDC, etc.) - recommended for compatible tokens\n * - permit2: Uses Permit2 + x402Permit2Proxy - universal fallback for any ERC-20\n */\ntype AssetTransferMethod = \"eip3009\" | \"permit2\";\n/**\n * EIP-3009 payload for tokens with native transferWithAuthorization support.\n */\ntype ExactEIP3009Payload = {\n    signature?: `0x${string}`;\n    authorization: {\n        from: `0x${string}`;\n        to: `0x${string}`;\n        value: string;\n        validAfter: string;\n        validBefore: string;\n        nonce: `0x${string}`;\n    };\n};\n/**\n * Permit2 witness data structure.\n * Matches the Witness struct in x402Permit2Proxy contract.\n * Note: Upper time bound is enforced by Permit2's `deadline` field, not a witness field.\n */\ntype Permit2Witness = {\n    to: `0x${string}`;\n    validAfter: string;\n};\n/**\n * Permit2 authorization parameters.\n * Used to reconstruct the signed message for verification.\n */\ntype Permit2Authorization = {\n    permitted: {\n        token: `0x${string}`;\n        amount: string;\n    };\n    spender: `0x${string}`;\n    nonce: string;\n    deadline: string;\n    witness: Permit2Witness;\n};\n/**\n * Permit2 payload for tokens using the Permit2 + x402Permit2Proxy flow.\n */\ntype ExactPermit2Payload = {\n    signature: `0x${string}`;\n    permit2Authorization: Permit2Authorization & {\n        from: `0x${string}`;\n    };\n};\ntype ExactEvmPayloadV1 = ExactEIP3009Payload;\ntype ExactEvmPayloadV2 = ExactEIP3009Payload | ExactPermit2Payload;\n/**\n * Type guard to check if a payload is a Permit2 payload.\n * Permit2 payloads have a `permit2Authorization` field.\n *\n * @param payload - The payload to check.\n * @returns True if the payload is a Permit2 payload, false otherwise.\n */\ndeclare function isPermit2Payload(payload: ExactEvmPayloadV2): payload is ExactPermit2Payload;\n/**\n * Type guard to check if a payload is an EIP-3009 payload.\n * EIP-3009 payloads have an `authorization` field.\n *\n * @param payload - parser - The money parser function to register\\n   * @returns This instance for chaining\\n   */\\n  registerMoneyParser(parser: MoneyParser): UptoEvmScheme {\\n    this.moneyParsers.push(parser);\\n Decimals when the asset is a known default; otherwise undefined\\n   */\\n  getAssetDecimals(asset: string, network: Network): number | undefined {\\n    return findDefaultAsset(asset, network)?.decimals;\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Parses a price into an asset amount for the given network.\\n   *\\n   * @param price - The price to parse (string, number, or AssetAmount)\\n   * @param network - The target network\\n   * @returns Promise resolving to an asset amount\\n   */\\n  async parsePrice(price: Price, network: Network): Promise<AssetAmount> {\\n    if (typeof price === \\\"object\\\" && price !== null && \\\"amount\\\" in price) {\\n      if (!price.asset) {\\n        throw new Error(`Asset address must be specified for AssetAmount on network ${network}`);\\n      }\\n      return {\\n        amount: price.amount,\\n        asset: price.asset,\\n        extra: price.extra || {},\\n      };\\n    }\\n\\n    const { amount, symbol } = parseMoney(price);\\n\\n    for (const parser of this.moneyParsers) {\\n      const result = await parser(amount, network);\\n      if (result !== null) {\\n        return result;\\n      }\\n    }\\n\\n    return this.defaultMoneyConversion(amount, network, symbol);\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Enhances payment requirements with upto-specific metadata.\\n   *\\n   * @param paymentRequirements - The base payment requirements\\n   * @param supportedKind - The supported scheme/network kind\\n   * @param supportedKind.x402Version - The x402 protocol version\\n   * @param supportedKind.scheme - The payment scheme name\\n   * @param supportedKind.network - The target network\\n   * @param supportedKind.extra - Optional extra metadata\\n   * @param extensionKeys - Extension keys to include\\n   * @returns Promise resolving to enhanced payment requirements\\n   */\\n  enhancePaymentRequirements(\\n    paymentRequirements: PaymentRequirements,\\n    supportedKind: {\\n      x402Version: number;\\n      scheme: string;\\n      network: Network;\\n      extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\\n    },\\n    extensionKeys: string[],\\n  ): Promise<PaymentRequirements> {\\n    void extensionKeys;\\n    return Promise.resolve({\\n      ...paymentRequirements,\\n      extra: {\\n        ...paymentRequirements.extra,\\n        assetTransferMethod: \\\"permit2\\\",\\n        ...(supportedKind.extra?.facilitatorAddress\\n          ? { facilitatorAddress: getAddress(supportedKind.extra.facilitatorAddress as string) }\\n          : {}),\\n      },\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Converts a decimal dollar amount to an AssetAmount using the default token for the network.\\n   *\\n   * @param amount - The decimal amount as a string\\n   * @param network - The target network\\n   * @param symbol - Optional ticker from a suffixed price\\n   * @returns The converted asset amount with token metadata\\n   */\\n  private defaultMoneyConversion(amount: string, network: amount: tokenAmount,\\n      asset: assetInfo.asset,\\n      extra: {\\n        name: assetInfo.name,\\n        version: assetInfo.version,\\n        assetTransferMethod: \\\"permit2\\\",\\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRv9ygHuCSQeMRLhSUDn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01C5y9QmdaoP7YqZ5q1Aawwo","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/server.ts","content":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — weather forecasts sold to autonomous agents.\n *\n *   $0.35 / call, native USDC on Base, x402 (exact / eip3009), no accounts.\n *   Identity + reputation: ERC-8004 on Base (eip155:8453).\n *\n * See design.md for the full architecture and the bootstrap steps that must be\n * completed onchain before this server is discoverable.\n */\n\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { join } from \"node:path\";\n\nimport express, { type Request, type Response } from \"express\";\nimport { paymentMiddleware } from \"@x402/express\";\nimport { HTTPFacilitatorClient, x402ResourceServer, type RoutesConfig } from \"@x402/core/server\";\nimport { decodePaymentSignatureHeader } from \"@x402/core/http\";\nimport { registerExactEvmScheme } from \"@x402/evm/exact/server\";\nimport { isEIP3009Payload, type ExactEvmPayloadV2 } from \"@x402/evm\";\nimport { createFacilitatorConfig } from \"@coinbase/x402\";\nimport { createPublicClient, http, parseAbi, getAddress, type Address } from \"viem\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Configuration\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** CAIP-2 id of the settlement chain. Base mainnet. */\nconst NETWORK = \"eip155:8453\" as const;\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\n\n/**\n * Native USDC on Base. NOT 0xA0b8...eB48 (that is Ethereum mainnet USDC) and\n * NOT 0xd9aA...b6CA (that is bridged USDbC). Only this contract implements the\n * EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization` that the gasless flow depends on here.\n */\nconst USDC_BASE = \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\" as const;\n\n/** $0.35 in USDC base units — USDC has 6 decimals, so 0.35 * 10^6. */\nconst PRICE_BASE_UNITS = \"350000\" as const;\n\n/** ERC-8004 registries. These are the *mainnet* pair; testnets use 0x8004A818… / 0x8004B663…. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" as const;\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = \"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\" as const;\n\n/** Chain-scoped registry reference, the form clients use to address this agent. */\nconst AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`;\n\nconst ORIGIN = env(\"ORIGIN\", \"https://forecast.example.com\");\nconst PORT = Number(env(\"PORT\", \"8402\"));\n\n/** tokenId minted by IdentityRegistry.register(agentURI). Assigned by the registry — never chosen. */\nconst AGENT_ID = env(\"AGENT_ID\");\n\n/** Where settled USDC lands. Must equal IdentityRegistry.getAgentWallet(AGENT_ID). */\nconst PAY_TO = getAddress(env(\"PAY_TO\"));\n\n/** agentURI stored onchain (immutable IPFS copy of the registration file). */\nconst AGENT_URI = env(\"AGENT_URI\", \"ipfs://bafkreiforecastexampleregistrationfileplaceholder\");\n\n/** Hot key used only to sign receipts. Never holds funds; announced onchain via setMetadata. */\nconst RECEIPT_SIGNER = privateKeyToAccount(\n  env(\"RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY\") as `0x${string}`,\n);\n\n/** Optional durable receipt directory. Receipts are referenced by onchain feedback — see design.md. */\nconst RECEIPT_DIR = process.env.RECEIPT_DIR;\n\nconst RPC_URL = env(\"BASE_RPC_URL\", \"https://mainnet.base.org\");\n\n/** Upstream forecast provider. Offchain dependency; no key required. */\nconst UPSTREAM = \"https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast\";\n\n/** The exact `endpoint` string callers should pass to ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback. */\nconst FEEDBACK_ENDPOINT = `GET ${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`;\n\nfunction env(name: string, fallback?: string): string {\n  const value = process.env[name] ?? fallback;\n  if (value === undefined) throw new Error(`missing required env var ${name}`);\n  return value;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Onchain reads (convenience mirror only — the registry is authoritative)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst publicClient = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\n\nconst reputationAbi = parseAbi([\n  \"function getSummary(uint256 agentId, address[] clientAddresses, string tag1, string tag2) view returns (uint64 count, int128 summaryValue, uint8 summaryValueDecimals)\",\n  \"function readAllFeedback(uint256 agentId, address[] clientAddresses, string tag1, string tag2, bool includeRevoked) view returns (address[] clients, uint64[] feedbackIndexes, int128[] values, uint8[] valueDecimals, string[] tag1s, string[] tag2s, bool[] revokedStatuses)\",\n  \"function getClients(uint256 agentId) view returns (address[])\",\n]);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Receipts\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * The immutable half of a receipt. This is what gets hashed and signed, so a\n * caller can compute `feedbackHash` the moment it gets its 200 — before the\n * settlement transaction has even been mined.\n */\ntype ReceiptCore = {\n  version: \"forecast.example.com/receipt-v1\";\n  receiptId: string;\n  agentId: string;\n  agentRegistry: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  resource: string;\n  payer: Address;\n  payTo: Address;\n  asset: typeof USDC_BASE;\n  amount: typeof PRICE_BASE_UNITS;\n  network: typeof NETWORK;\n  requestParams: Record<string, string>;\n  responseHash: string;\n  servedAt: string;\n};\n\ntype Receipt = {\n  receipt: ReceiptCore;\n  receiptHash: `0x${string}`;\n  /** EIP-191 signature over the canonical receipt core, by the announced receipt signer. */\n  signature: `0x${string}`;\n  signer: Address;\n  /** Filled in by the afterSettle hook; excluded from the hash so the hash is stable immediately. */\n  settlement: { status: \"pending\" | \"settled\" | \"failed\"; transaction?: string; error?: string };\n};\n\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n/** authorization nonce -> receiptId, so the settle hook can find the receipt it belongs to. */\nconst receiptByNonce = new Map<string, string>();\n\nif (RECEIPT_DIR) mkdirSync(RECEIPT_DIR, { recursive: true });\n\n/** Deterministic JSON: sorted keys, no whitespace. Both sides must hash the same bytes. */\nfunction canonicalize(value: unknown): string {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== \"object\") return JSON.stringify(value);\n  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalize).join(\",\")}]`;\n  const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)\n    .filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)\n    .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : 1));\n  return `{${entries.map(([k, v]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonicalize(v)}`).join(\",\")}}`;\n}\n\nfunction sha256Hex(input: string): `0x${string}` {\n  return `0x${createHash(\"sha256\").update(input).digest(\"hex\")}`;\n}\n\nfunction saveReceipt(receipt: Receipt): void {\n  receipts.set(receipt.receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n  if (RECEIPT_DIR) {\n    writeFileSync(join(RECEIPT_DIR, `${receipt.receipt.receiptId}.json`), JSON.stringify(receipt));\n  }\n}\n\nfunction loadReceipt(receiptId: string): Receipt | undefined {\n  const cached = receipts.get(receiptId);\n  if (cached) return cached;\n  if (!RECEIPT_DIR || !/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(receiptId)) return undefined;\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(RECEIPT_DIR, `${receiptId}.json`), \"utf8\")) as Receipt;\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Pull the payer address and the EIP-3009 nonce straight out of the payment\n * header. The middleware has already verified the payment by the time a\n * protected handler runs, so this is a decode, not a trust decision.\n */\nfunction readPayment(req: Request): { payer: Address; nonce?: string } | undefined {\n  const header = req.header(\"payment-signature\") ?? req.header(\"x-payment\");\n  if (!header) return undefined;\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodePaymentSignatureHeader(header);\n    const payload = decoded.payload as unknown as ExactEvmPayloadV2;\n    if (isEIP3009Payload(payload)) {\n      return { payer: getAddress(payload.authorization.from), nonce: payload.authorization.nonce };\n    }\n    return { payer: getAddress(payload.permit2Authorization.from) };\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Upstream forecast\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntype ForecastQuery = { latitude: number; longitude: number; hours: number };\n\nfunction parseQuery(req: Request): ForecastQuery | { error: string } {\n  const latitude = Number(req.query.lat);\n  const longitude = Number(req.query.lon);\n  const hours = req.query.hours === undefined ? 24 : Number(req.query.hours);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(latitude) || latitude < -90 || latitude > 90) {\n    return { error: \"lat must be a number in [-90, 90]\" };\n  }\n  if (!Number.isFinite(longitude) || longitude < -180 || longitude > 180) {\n    return { error: \"lon must be a number in [-180, 180]\" };\n  }\n  if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) {\n    return { error: \"hours must be an integer in [1, 168]\" };\n  }\n  return { latitude, longitude, hours };\n}\n\nasync function fetchForecast(query: ForecastQuery): Promise<unknown> {\n  const url = new URL(UPSTREAM);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(query.latitude));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(query.longitude));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(query.hours));\n  url.searchParams.set(\n    \"hourly\",\n    \"temperature_2m,precipitation,precipitation_probability,wind_speed_10m,weather_code\",\n  );\n\n  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8_000) });\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${response.status}`);\n  return response.json();\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 wiring\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Coinbase runs a public facilitator; anyone can run their own and point\n * FACILITATOR_URL at it. The facilitator is what submits the EIP-3009\n * authorization onchain and pays the gas — which is why neither the caller nor\n * this server needs to hold ETH.\n */\nconst facilitatorConfig = process.env.FACILITATOR_URL\n  ? { url: process.env.FACILITATOR_URL }\n  : createFacilitatorConfig(process.env.CDP_API_KEY_ID, process.env.CDP_API_KEY_SECRET);\n\nconst resourceServer = new x402ResourceServer(new HTTPFacilitatorClient(facilitatorConfig));\nregisterExactEvmScheme(resourceServer, { networks: [NETWORK] });\n\n/**\n * Settlement happens after the handler returns, so the receipt is written first\n * and its settlement status patched here. The caller also receives the same\n * transaction hash directly in the PAYMENT-RESPONSE header.\n */\nresourceServer.onAfterSettle(async ({ paymentPayload, result }) => {\n  const payload = paymentPayload.payload as unknown as ExactEvmPayloadV2;\n  if (!isEIP3009Payload(payload)) return;\n  const receiptId = receiptByNonce.get(payload.authorization.nonce);\n  if (!receiptId) return;\n  receiptByNonce.delete(payload.authorization.nonce);\n\n  const receipt = loadReceipt(receiptId);\n  if (!receipt) return;\n  receipt.settlement = result.success\n    ? { status: \"settled\", transaction: result.transaction }\n    : { status: \"failed\", error: result.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\" };\n  saveReceipt(receipt);\n});\n\n/**\n * Body returned with the 402 for non-browser clients. The 402 is the price\n * oracle: a cold client learns the price, the asset and the identity to check\n * without ever paying, and without reading prose.\n */\nconst unpaidResponseBody = () => ({\n  contentType: \"application/json\",\n  body: {\n    service: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    price: { amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS, asset: USDC_BASE, network: NETWORK, human: \"$0.35 USDC\" },\n    identity: { agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP, agentId: AGENT_ID, agentURI: AGENT_URI },\n    reputation: {\n      registry: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      read: \"readAllFeedback(agentId, clientAddresses, tag1, tag2, includeRevoked)\",\n      note: \"getSummary reverts on an empty clientAddresses list — pass the clients you trust.\",\n    },\n    docs: `${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-card.json`,\n    parameters: { lat: \"required, [-90,90]\", lon: \"required, [-180,180]\", hours: \"optional, 1-168, default 24\" },\n  },\n});\n\nconst routes: RoutesConfig = {\n  \"GET /v1/forecast\": {\n    accepts: [\n      {\n        scheme: \"exact\",\n        network: NETWORK,\n        payTo: PAY_TO,\n        // Pinned as an AssetAmount rather than \"$0.35\": the asset address and the\n        // base-unit amount are stated explicitly, and `extra` carries the EIP-712\n        // domain the client needs to sign the transferWithAuthorization.\n        price: {\n          asset: USDC_BASE,\n          amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS,\n          extra: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" },\n        },\n        maxTimeoutSeconds: 120,\n      },\n    ],\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    description: \"Hourly weather forecast (up to 168h) for a lat/lon, as JSON.\",\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    serviceName: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"data\"],\n    unpaidResponseBody,\n  },\n};\n\nconst app = express();\napp.disable(\"x-powered-by\");\napp.use(express.json({ limit: \"16kb\" }));\n\n// Only GET /v1/forecast is priced; every other route falls through unpriced.\napp.use(paymentMiddleware(routes, resourceServer));\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Discovery documents (free, cacheable, machine-readable)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * ERC-8004 endpoint-domain binding. The agentURI lives on IPFS, so it is *not*\n * served from this domain — which means control of this domain is unproven\n * unless this file exists here. An A2A agent-card.json is a different document\n * and does not do this job.\n */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=300\");\n  res.json({\n    type: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Per-call weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35 USDC on Base, x402.\",\n    registrations: [{ agentId: Number(AGENT_ID), agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP }],\n    services: [\n      { name: \"x402\", endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast` },\n      { name: \"A2A\", endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-card.json` },\n      { name: \"agentWallet\", endpoint: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${PAY_TO}` },\n      { name: \"receiptSigner\", endpoint: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${RECEIPT_SIGNER.address}` },\n    ],\n    x402Support: true,\n    active: true,\n    supportedTrust: [\"reputation\"],\n  });\n});\n\n/** A2A card — capabilities and price, for clients that speak A2A. Not a trust anchor. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-card.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=300\");\n  res.json({\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Hourly weather forecasts sold per call to autonomous agents.\",\n    url: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    provider: { organization: \"forecast.example.com\", url: ORIGIN },\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, pushNotifications: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"hourly-forecast\",\n        name: \"Hourly forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly temperature, precipitation, precipitation probability and wind for a lat/lon.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\"],\n        examples: [`${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast?lat=52.37&lon=4.90&hours=24`],\n      },\n    ],\n    registrations: [{ agentId: Number(AGENT_ID), agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP }],\n    x402: {\n      network: NETWORK,\n      asset: USDC_BASE,\n      amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS,\n      payTo: PAY_TO,\n      assetTransferMethod: \"eip3009\",\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The priced endpoint\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\"/v1/forecast\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const query = parseQuery(req);\n  if (\"error\" in query) {\n    // 4xx before the settle step: the middleware cancels the verified payment,\n    // so a malformed request is never charged.\n    res.status(400).json({ error: query.error });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let forecast: unknown;\n  try {\n    forecast = await fetchForecast(query);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Same contract on upstream failure: 502 means no settlement, no charge.\n    res.status(502).json({ error: \"upstream forecast provider unavailable\" });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const payment = readPayment(req);\n  const receiptId = createHash(\"sha256\")\n    .update(`${payment?.nonce ?? \"\"}:${req.originalUrl}:${Date.now()}`)\n    .digest(\"hex\")\n    .slice(0, 32);\n\n  const core: ReceiptCore = {\n    version: \"forecast.example.com/receipt-v1\",\n    receiptId,\n    agentId: AGENT_ID,\n    agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n    endpoint: FEEDBACK_ENDPOINT,\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    payer: payment?.payer ?? (\"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\" as Address),\n    payTo: PAY_TO,\n    asset: USDC_BASE,\n    amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS,\n    network: NETWORK,\n    requestParams: {\n      lat: String(query.latitude),\n      lon: String(query.longitude),\n      hours: String(query.hours),\n    },\n    responseHash: sha256Hex(canonicalize(forecast)),\n    servedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  };\n\n  const canonicalCore = canonicalize(core);\n  const receiptHash = sha256Hex(canonicalCore);\n  const receipt: Receipt = {\n    receipt: core,\n    receiptHash,\n    signature: await RECEIPT_SIGNER.signMessage({ message: canonicalCore }),\n    signer: RECEIPT_SIGNER.address,\n    settlement: { status: \"pending\" },\n  };\n  saveReceipt(receipt);\n  if (payment?.nonce) receiptByNonce.set(payment.nonce, receiptId);\n\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"private, no-store\");\n  res.json({\n    forecast,\n    receipt: {\n      receiptId,\n      receiptURI: `${ORIGIN}/receipts/${receiptId}`,\n      receiptHash,\n    },\n    // Everything the caller needs to rate this call, as literal call arguments.\n    // No lookup, no docs, no human.\n    feedback: {\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      registry: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      function:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint, string feedbackURI, bytes32 feedbackHash)\",\n      args: {\n        agentId: AGENT_ID,\n        value: \"<0-100, your score>\",\n        valueDecimals: 0,\n        tag1: \"forecast-quality\",\n        tag2: \"latency\",\n        endpoint: FEEDBACK_ENDPOINT,\n        feedbackURI: `${ORIGIN}/receipts/${receiptId}`,\n        feedbackHash: receiptHash,\n      },\n      note: \"The registry rejects feedback from this agent's owner and operators, so these ratings cannot be self-issued.\",\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Receipts and reputation (free)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\"/receipts/:id\", (req, res) => {\n  const receipt = loadReceipt(req.params.id);\n  if (!receipt) {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n    return;\n  }\n  // The signed core is immutable; only `settlement` changes, and it is outside the hash.\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=60\");\n  res.json(receipt);\n});\n\n/**\n * Convenience mirror of what any client can read for itself from the\n * ReputationRegistry. Explicitly NOT authoritative — a rating that only exists\n * here is worthless, which is the point of putting them onchain.\n */\napp.get(\"/reputation\", async (req, res) => {\n  const clientsParam = typeof req.query.clients === \"string\" ? req.query.clients : \"\";\n  const clients = clientsParam\n    .split(\",\")\n    .map((value) => value.trim())\n    .filter(Boolean)\n    .map((value) => getAddress(value));\n  const tag1 = typeof req.query.tag1 === \"string\" ? req.query.tag1 : \"\";\n  const tag2 = typeof req.query.tag2 === \"string\" ? req.query.tag2 : \"\";\n\n  try {\n    const source = {\n      authoritative: false,\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      registry: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      note: \"Read this from the chain yourself; this endpoint is a cache served by the rated party.\",\n    };\n\n    if (clients.length > 0) {\n      const [count, summaryValue, summaryValueDecimals] = await publicClient.readContract({\n        address: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n        abi: reputationAbi,\n        functionName: \"getSummary\",\n        args: [BigInt(AGENT_ID), clients, tag1, tag2],\n      });\n      res.json({\n        ...source,\n        filteredTo: clients,\n        count: Number(count),\n        value: Number(summaryValue),\n        valueDecimals: summaryValueDecimals,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // getSummary reverts with \"clientAddresses required\" on an empty list, so an\n    // unfiltered view has to go through readAllFeedback — and carries a warning.\n    const [feedbackClients, indexes, values, decimals, tag1s, tag2s, revoked] =\n      await publicClient.readContract({\n        address: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n        abi: reputationAbi,\n        functionName: \"readAllFeedback\",\n        args: [BigInt(AGENT_ID), [], tag1, tag2, false],\n      });\n    res.json({\n      ...source,\n      warning:\n        \"Unfiltered totals are Sybil bait. Pass ?clients=0x…,0x… to score only raters you trust.\",\n      feedback: feedbackClients.map((client, i) => ({\n        client,\n        index: Number(indexes[i]),\n        value: Number(values[i]),\n        valueDecimals: decimals[i],\n        tag1: tag1s[i],\n        tag2: tag2s[i],\n        revoked: revoked[i],\n      })),\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    res.status(503).json({ error: \"reputation read failed\", detail: String(error) });\n  }\n});\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req, res) => res.json({ ok: true, agentId: AGENT_ID, network: NETWORK }));\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${PORT}`);\n  console.log(`  agent   ${AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP} #${AGENT_ID}`);\n  console.log(`  price   ${PRICE_BASE_UNITS} base units USDC (${USDC_BASE}) on ${NETWORK}`);\n  console.log(`  payTo   ${PAY_TO}`);\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2096,"cache_read_input_tokens":80938,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2096},"output_tokens":14,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"7caec005-c2b3-4b4b-a8ea-7ab242769fdf","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:33:49.730Z","request_id":"req_011CeRv9xgkvwiTW7B7gxygG"}

back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"eab5c533-063b-4099-90ec-e565d13af6c2","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:33:49.742Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/server.ts","content":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — weather forecasts sold to autonomous agents.\n *\n *   $0.35 / call, native USDC on Base, x402 (exact / eip3009), no accounts.\n *   Identity + reputation: ERC-8004 on Base (eip155:8453).\n *\n * See design.md for the full architecture and the bootstrap steps that must be\n * completed onchain before this server is discoverable.\n */\n\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { join } from \"node:path\";\n\nimport express, { type Request, type Response } from \"express\";\nimport { paymentMiddleware } from \"@x402/express\";\nimport { HTTPFacilitatorClient, x402ResourceServer, type RoutesConfig } from \"@x402/core/server\";\nimport { decodePaymentSignatureHeader } from \"@x402/core/http\";\nimport { registerExactEvmScheme } from \"@x402/evm/exact/server\";\nimport { isEIP3009Payload, type ExactEvmPayloadV2 } from \"@x402/evm\";\nimport { createFacilitatorConfig } from \"@coinbase/x402\";\nimport { createPublicClient, http, parseAbi, getAddress, type Address } from \"viem\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Configuration\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** CAIP-2 id of the settlement chain. Base mainnet. */\nconst NETWORK = \"eip155:8453\" as const;\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\n\n/**\n * Native USDC on Base. NOT 0xA0b8...eB48 (that is Ethereum mainnet USDC) and\n * NOT 0xd9aA...b6CA (that is bridged USDbC). Only this contract implements the\n * EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization` that the gasless flow depends on here.\n */\nconst USDC_BASE = \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\" as const;\n\n/** $0.35 in USDC base units — USDC has 6 decimals, so 0.35 * 10^6. */\nconst PRICE_BASE_UNITS = \"350000\" as const;\n\n/** ERC-8004 registries. These are the *mainnet* pair; testnets use 0x8004A818… / 0x8004B663…. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" as const;\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY = \"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\" as const;\n\n/** Chain-scoped registry reference, the form clients use to address this agent. */\nconst AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`;\n\nconst ORIGIN = env(\"ORIGIN\", \"https://forecast.example.com\");\nconst PORT = Number(env(\"PORT\", \"8402\"));\n\n/** tokenId minted by IdentityRegistry.register(agentURI). Assigned by the registry — never chosen. */\nconst AGENT_ID = env(\"AGENT_ID\");\n\n/** Where settled USDC lands. Must equal IdentityRegistry.getAgentWallet(AGENT_ID). */\nconst PAY_TO = getAddress(env(\"PAY_TO\"));\n\n/** agentURI stored onchain (immutable IPFS copy of the registration file). */\nconst AGENT_URI = env(\"AGENT_URI\", \"ipfs://bafkreiforecastexampleregistrationfileplaceholder\");\n\n/** Hot key used only to sign receipts. Never holds funds; announced onchain via setMetadata. */\nconst RECEIPT_SIGNER = privateKeyToAccount(\n  env(\"RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY\") as `0x${string}`,\n);\n\n/** Optional durable receipt directory. Receipts are referenced by onchain feedback — see design.md. */\nconst RECEIPT_DIR = process.env.RECEIPT_DIR;\n\nconst RPC_URL = env(\"BASE_RPC_URL\", \"https://mainnet.base.org\");\n\n/** Upstream forecast provider. Offchain dependency; no key required. */\nconst UPSTREAM = \"https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast\";\n\n/** The exact `endpoint` string callers should pass to ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback. */\nconst FEEDBACK_ENDPOINT = `GET ${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`;\n\nfunction env(name: string, fallback?: string): string {\n  const value = process.env[name] ?? fallback;\n  if (value === undefined) throw new Error(`missing required env var ${name}`);\n  return value;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Onchain reads (convenience mirror only — the registry is authoritative)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst publicClient = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\n\nconst reputationAbi = parseAbi([\n  \"function getSummary(uint256 agentId, address[] clientAddresses, string tag1, string tag2) view returns (uint64 count, int128 summaryValue, uint8 summaryValueDecimals)\",\n  \"function readAllFeedback(uint256 agentId, address[] clientAddresses, string tag1, string tag2, bool includeRevoked) view returns (address[] clients, uint64[] feedbackIndexes, int128[] values, uint8[] valueDecimals, string[] tag1s, string[] tag2s, bool[] revokedStatuses)\",\n  \"function getClients(uint256 agentId) view returns (address[])\",\n]);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Receipts\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * The immutable half of a receipt. This is what gets hashed and signed, so a\n * caller can compute `feedbackHash` the moment it gets its 200 — before the\n * settlement transaction has even been mined.\n */\ntype ReceiptCore = {\n  version: \"forecast.example.com/receipt-v1\";\n  receiptId: string;\n  agentId: string;\n  agentRegistry: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  resource: string;\n  payer: Address;\n  payTo: Address;\n  asset: typeof USDC_BASE;\n  amount: typeof PRICE_BASE_UNITS;\n  network: typeof NETWORK;\n  requestParams: Record<string, string>;\n  responseHash: string;\n  servedAt: string;\n};\n\ntype Receipt = {\n  receipt: ReceiptCore;\n  receiptHash: `0x${string}`;\n  /** EIP-191 signature over the canonical receipt core, by the announced receipt signer. */\n  signature: `0x${string}`;\n  signer: Address;\n  /** Filled in by the afterSettle hook; excluded from the hash so the hash is stable immediately. */\n  settlement: { status: \"pending\" | \"settled\" | \"failed\"; transaction?: string; error?: string };\n};\n\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n/** authorization nonce -> receiptId, so the settle hook can find the receipt it belongs to. */\nconst receiptByNonce = new Map<string, string>();\n\nif (RECEIPT_DIR) mkdirSync(RECEIPT_DIR, { recursive: true });\n\n/** Deterministic JSON: sorted keys, no whitespace. Both sides must hash the same bytes. */\nfunction canonicalize(value: unknown): string {\n  if (value === null || typeof value !== \"object\") return JSON.stringify(value);\n  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalize).join(\",\")}]`;\n  const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)\n    .filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)\n    .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : 1));\n  return `{${entries.map(([k, v]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonicalize(v)}`).join(\",\")}}`;\n}\n\nfunction sha256Hex(input: string): `0x${string}` {\n  return `0x${createHash(\"sha256\").update(input).digest(\"hex\")}`;\n}\n\nfunction saveReceipt(receipt: Receipt): void {\n  receipts.set(receipt.receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n  if (RECEIPT_DIR) {\n    writeFileSync(join(RECEIPT_DIR, `${receipt.receipt.receiptId}.json`), JSON.stringify(receipt));\n  }\n}\n\nfunction loadReceipt(receiptId: string): Receipt | undefined {\n  const cached = receipts.get(receiptId);\n  if (cached) return cached;\n  if (!RECEIPT_DIR || !/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(receiptId)) return undefined;\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(RECEIPT_DIR, `${receiptId}.json`), \"utf8\")) as Receipt;\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Pull the payer address and the EIP-3009 nonce straight out of the payment\n * header. The middleware has already verified the payment by the time a\n * protected handler runs, so this is a decode, not a trust decision.\n */\nfunction readPayment(req: Request): { payer: Address; nonce?: string } | undefined {\n  const header = req.header(\"payment-signature\") ?? req.header(\"x-payment\");\n  if (!header) return undefined;\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodePaymentSignatureHeader(header);\n    const payload = decoded.payload as unknown as ExactEvmPayloadV2;\n    if (isEIP3009Payload(payload)) {\n      return { payer: getAddress(payload.authorization.from), nonce: payload.authorization.nonce };\n    }\n    return { payer: getAddress(payload.permit2Authorization.from) };\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Upstream forecast\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntype ForecastQuery = { latitude: number; longitude: number; hours: number };\n\nfunction parseQuery(req: Request): ForecastQuery | { error: string } {\n  const latitude = Number(req.query.lat);\n  const longitude = Number(req.query.lon);\n  const hours = req.query.hours === undefined ? 24 : Number(req.query.hours);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(latitude) || latitude < -90 || latitude > 90) {\n    return { error: \"lat must be a number in [-90, 90]\" };\n  }\n  if (!Number.isFinite(longitude) || longitude < -180 || longitude > 180) {\n    return { error: \"lon must be a number in [-180, 180]\" };\n  }\n  if (!Number.isInteger(hours) || hours < 1 || hours > 168) {\n    return { error: \"hours must be an integer in [1, 168]\" };\n  }\n  return { latitude, longitude, hours };\n}\n\nasync function fetchForecast(query: ForecastQuery): Promise<unknown> {\n  const url = new URL(UPSTREAM);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(query.latitude));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(query.longitude));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(query.hours));\n  url.searchParams.set(\n    \"hourly\",\n    \"temperature_2m,precipitation,precipitation_probability,wind_speed_10m,weather_code\",\n  );\n\n  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8_000) });\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${response.status}`);\n  return response.json();\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// x402 wiring\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Coinbase runs a public facilitator; anyone can run their own and point\n * FACILITATOR_URL at it. The facilitator is what submits the EIP-3009\n * authorization onchain and pays the gas — which is why neither the caller nor\n * this server needs to hold ETH.\n */\nconst facilitatorConfig = process.env.FACILITATOR_URL\n  ? { url: process.env.FACILITATOR_URL }\n  : createFacilitatorConfig(process.env.CDP_API_KEY_ID, process.env.CDP_API_KEY_SECRET);\n\nconst resourceServer = new x402ResourceServer(new HTTPFacilitatorClient(facilitatorConfig));\nregisterExactEvmScheme(resourceServer, { networks: [NETWORK] });\n\n/**\n * Settlement happens after the handler returns, so the receipt is written first\n * and its settlement status patched here. The caller also receives the same\n * transaction hash directly in the PAYMENT-RESPONSE header.\n */\nresourceServer.onAfterSettle(async ({ paymentPayload, result }) => {\n  const payload = paymentPayload.payload as unknown as ExactEvmPayloadV2;\n  if (!isEIP3009Payload(payload)) return;\n  const receiptId = receiptByNonce.get(payload.authorization.nonce);\n  if (!receiptId) return;\n  receiptByNonce.delete(payload.authorization.nonce);\n\n  const receipt = loadReceipt(receiptId);\n  if (!receipt) return;\n  receipt.settlement = result.success\n    ? { status: \"settled\", transaction: result.transaction }\n    : { status: \"failed\", error: result.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\" };\n  saveReceipt(receipt);\n});\n\n/**\n * Body returned with the 402 for non-browser clients. The 402 is the price\n * oracle: a cold client learns the price, the asset and the identity to check\n * without ever paying, and without reading prose.\n */\nconst unpaidResponseBody = () => ({\n  contentType: \"application/json\",\n  body: {\n    service: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    price: { amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS, asset: USDC_BASE, network: NETWORK, human: \"$0.35 USDC\" },\n    identity: { agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP, agentId: AGENT_ID, agentURI: AGENT_URI },\n    reputation: {\n      registry: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      read: \"readAllFeedback(agentId, clientAddresses, tag1, tag2, includeRevoked)\",\n      note: \"getSummary reverts on an empty clientAddresses list — pass the clients you trust.\",\n    },\n    docs: `${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-card.json`,\n    parameters: { lat: \"required, [-90,90]\", lon: \"required, [-180,180]\", hours: \"optional, 1-168, default 24\" },\n  },\n});\n\nconst routes: RoutesConfig = {\n  \"GET /v1/forecast\": {\n    accepts: [\n      {\n        scheme: \"exact\",\n        network: NETWORK,\n        payTo: PAY_TO,\n        // Pinned as an AssetAmount rather than \"$0.35\": the asset address and the\n        // base-unit amount are stated explicitly, and `extra` carries the EIP-712\n        // domain the client needs to sign the transferWithAuthorization.\n        price: {\n          asset: USDC_BASE,\n          amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS,\n          extra: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" },\n        },\n        maxTimeoutSeconds: 120,\n      },\n    ],\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    description: \"Hourly weather forecast (up to 168h) for a lat/lon, as JSON.\",\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    serviceName: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"data\"],\n    unpaidResponseBody,\n  },\n};\n\nconst app = express();\napp.disable(\"x-powered-by\");\napp.use(express.json({ limit: \"16kb\" }));\n\n// Only GET /v1/forecast is priced; every other route falls through unpriced.\napp.use(paymentMiddleware(routes, resourceServer));\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Discovery documents (free, cacheable, machine-readable)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * ERC-8004 endpoint-domain binding. The agentURI lives on IPFS, so it is *not*\n * served from this domain — which means control of this domain is unproven\n * unless this file exists here. An A2A agent-card.json is a different document\n * and does not do this job.\n */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=300\");\n  res.json({\n    type: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Per-call weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35 USDC on Base, x402.\",\n    registrations: [{ agentId: Number(AGENT_ID), agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP }],\n    services: [\n      { name: \"x402\", endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast` },\n      { name: \"A2A\", endpoint: `${ORIGIN}/.well-known/agent-card.json` },\n      { name: \"agentWallet\", endpoint: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${PAY_TO}` },\n      { name: \"receiptSigner\", endpoint: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${RECEIPT_SIGNER.address}` },\n    ],\n    x402Support: true,\n    active: true,\n    supportedTrust: [\"reputation\"],\n  });\n});\n\n/** A2A card — capabilities and price, for clients that speak A2A. Not a trust anchor. */\napp.get(\"/.well-known/agent-card.json\", (_req, res) => {\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=300\");\n  res.json({\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Hourly weather forecasts sold per call to autonomous agents.\",\n    url: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    provider: { organization: \"forecast.example.com\", url: ORIGIN },\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, pushNotifications: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"hourly-forecast\",\n        name: \"Hourly forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly temperature, precipitation, precipitation probability and wind for a lat/lon.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\"],\n        examples: [`${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast?lat=52.37&lon=4.90&hours=24`],\n      },\n    ],\n    registrations: [{ agentId: Number(AGENT_ID), agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP }],\n    x402: {\n      network: NETWORK,\n      asset: USDC_BASE,\n      amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS,\n      payTo: PAY_TO,\n      assetTransferMethod: \"eip3009\",\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The priced endpoint\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\"/v1/forecast\", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {\n  const query = parseQuery(req);\n  if (\"error\" in query) {\n    // 4xx before the settle step: the middleware cancels the verified payment,\n    // so a malformed request is never charged.\n    res.status(400).json({ error: query.error });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let forecast: unknown;\n  try {\n    forecast = await fetchForecast(query);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Same contract on upstream failure: 502 means no settlement, no charge.\n    res.status(502).json({ error: \"upstream forecast provider unavailable\" });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const payment = readPayment(req);\n  const receiptId = createHash(\"sha256\")\n    .update(`${payment?.nonce ?? \"\"}:${req.originalUrl}:${Date.now()}`)\n    .digest(\"hex\")\n    .slice(0, 32);\n\n  const core: ReceiptCore = {\n    version: \"forecast.example.com/receipt-v1\",\n    receiptId,\n    agentId: AGENT_ID,\n    agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n    endpoint: FEEDBACK_ENDPOINT,\n    resource: `${ORIGIN}/v1/forecast`,\n    payer: payment?.payer ?? (\"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\" as Address),\n    payTo: PAY_TO,\n    asset: USDC_BASE,\n    amount: PRICE_BASE_UNITS,\n    network: NETWORK,\n    requestParams: {\n      lat: String(query.latitude),\n      lon: String(query.longitude),\n      hours: String(query.hours),\n    },\n    responseHash: sha256Hex(canonicalize(forecast)),\n    servedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  };\n\n  const canonicalCore = canonicalize(core);\n  const receiptHash = sha256Hex(canonicalCore);\n  const receipt: Receipt = {\n    receipt: core,\n    receiptHash,\n    signature: await RECEIPT_SIGNER.signMessage({ message: canonicalCore }),\n    signer: RECEIPT_SIGNER.address,\n    settlement: { status: \"pending\" },\n  };\n  saveReceipt(receipt);\n  if (payment?.nonce) receiptByNonce.set(payment.nonce, receiptId);\n\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"private, no-store\");\n  res.json({\n    forecast,\n    receipt: {\n      receiptId,\n      receiptURI: `${ORIGIN}/receipts/${receiptId}`,\n      receiptHash,\n    },\n    // Everything the caller needs to rate this call, as literal call arguments.\n    // No lookup, no docs, no human.\n    feedback: {\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      registry: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      function:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals, string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint, string feedbackURI, bytes32 feedbackHash)\",\n      args: {\n        agentId: AGENT_ID,\n        value: \"<0-100, your score>\",\n        valueDecimals: 0,\n        tag1: \"forecast-quality\",\n        tag2: \"latency\",\n        endpoint: FEEDBACK_ENDPOINT,\n        feedbackURI: `${ORIGIN}/receipts/${receiptId}`,\n        feedbackHash: receiptHash,\n      },\n      note: \"The registry rejects feedback from this agent's owner and operators, so these ratings cannot be self-issued.\",\n    },\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Receipts and reputation (free)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\napp.get(\"/receipts/:id\", (req, res) => {\n  const receipt = loadReceipt(req.params.id);\n  if (!receipt) {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n    return;\n  }\n  // The signed core is immutable; only `settlement` changes, and it is outside the hash.\n  res.set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=60\");\n  res.json(receipt);\n});\n\n/**\n * Convenience mirror of what any client can read for itself from the\n * ReputationRegistry. Explicitly NOT authoritative — a rating that only exists\n * here is worthless, which is the point of putting them onchain.\n */\napp.get(\"/reputation\", async (req, res) => {\n  const clientsParam = typeof req.query.clients === \"string\" ? req.query.clients : \"\";\n  const clients = clientsParam\n    .split(\",\")\n    .map((value) => value.trim())\n    .filter(Boolean)\n    .map((value) => getAddress(value));\n  const tag1 = typeof req.query.tag1 === \"string\" ? req.query.tag1 : \"\";\n  const tag2 = typeof req.query.tag2 === \"string\" ? req.query.tag2 : \"\";\n\n  try {\n    const source = {\n      authoritative: false,\n      chainId: CHAIN_ID,\n      registry: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      agentId: AGENT_ID,\n      note: \"Read this from the chain yourself; this endpoint is a cache served by the rated party.\",\n    };\n\n    if (clients.length > 0) {\n      const [count, summaryValue, summaryValueDecimals] = await publicClient.readContract({\n        address: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n        abi: reputationAbi,\n        functionName: \"getSummary\",\n        args: [BigInt(AGENT_ID), clients, tag1, tag2],\n      });\n      res.json({\n        ...source,\n        filteredTo: clients,\n        count: Number(count),\n        value: Number(summaryValue),\n        valueDecimals: summaryValueDecimals,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // getSummary reverts with \"clientAddresses required\" on an empty list, so an\n    // unfiltered view has to go through readAllFeedback — and carries a warning.\n    const [feedbackClients, indexes, values, decimals, tag1s, tag2s, revoked] =\n      await publicClient.readContract({\n        address: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n        abi: reputationAbi,\n        functionName: \"readAllFeedback\",\n        args: [BigInt(AGENT_ID), [], tag1, tag2, false],\n      });\n    res.json({\n      ...source,\n      warning:\n        \"Unfiltered totals are Sybil bait. Pass ?clients=0x…,0x… to score only raters you trust.\",\n      feedback: feedbackClients.map((client, i) => ({\n        client,\n        index: Number(indexes[i]),\n        value: Number(values[i]),\n        valueDecimals: decimals[i],\n        tag1: tag1s[i],\n        tag2: tag2s[i],\n        revoked: revoked[i],\n      })),\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    res.status(503).json({ error: \"reputation read failed\", detail: String(error) });\n  }\n});\n\napp.get(\"/health\", (_req, res) => res.json({ ok: true, agentId: AGENT_ID, network: NETWORK }));\n\napp.listen(PORT, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${PORT}`);\n  console.log(`  agent   ${AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP} #${AGENT_ID}`);\n  console.log(`  price   ${PRICE_BASE_UNITS} base units USDC (${USDC_BASE}) on ${NETWORK}`);\n  console.log(`  payTo   ${PAY_TO}`);\n});\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
