{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvNsx7SQtzMnaDChfzP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now a mock-facilitator smoke test to exercise the paid path, receipt, and settle hook:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1182,"cache_read_input_tokens":101812,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1182},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"8c03212d-fd0b-4248-8d41-bb7515491cc0","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:35:14.318Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvNrtsA6z1v6HAgbeTU"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvNsx7SQtzMnaDChfzP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PrySMckJzgoEMkuiHQZq5z","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"W=/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace; cd $W && cat > smoke.ts <<'EOF'\n// Throwaway end-to-end check: mock facilitator + real client, Base Sepolia shape.\nimport express from \"express\";\nimport { x402Client } from \"@x402/core/client\";\nimport { wrapFetchWithPayment } from \"@x402/fetch\";\nimport { ExactEvmScheme } from \"@x402/evm\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\n\nconst fac = express();\nfac.use(express.json());\nfac.get(\"/supported\", (_r, s) => s.json({ kinds: [{ x402Version: 2, scheme: \"exact\", network: \"eip155:84532\" }] }));\nfac.post(\"/verify\", (req, s) => s.json({ isValid: true, payer: req.body.paymentPayload.payload.authorization.from }));\nfac.post(\"/settle\", (req, s) => s.json({ success: true, transaction: \"0x\" + \"ab\".repeat(32), network: \"eip155:84532\", payer: req.body.paymentPayload.payload.authorization.from }));\nawait new Promise<void>((r) => { fac.listen(8500, r); });\n\nconst account = privateKeyToAccount(\"0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\");\nconst client = new x402Client().register(\"eip155:84532\", new ExactEvmScheme(account));\nconst pay = wrapFetchWithPayment(fetch, client);\n\nconst res = await pay(\"http://localhost:8403/v1/forecast?lat=52.37&lon=4.90&hours=3\");\nconsole.log(\"status\", res.status);\nconst body: any = await res.json();\nconsole.log(\"payment-response hdr:\", (res.headers.get(\"payment-response\") ?? \"\").slice(0, 24), \"...\");\nconsole.log(\"receipt:\", body.receipt);\nconsole.log(\"feedback args:\", body.feedback?.args);\nconsole.log(\"forecast keys:\", Object.keys(body.forecast ?? {}));\nconst rec: any = await (await fetch(body.receipt.receiptURI)).json();\nconsole.log(\"stored receipt payer:\", rec.receipt.payer, \"settlement:\", rec.settlement);\nconsole.log(\"hash matches:\", rec.receiptHash === body.receipt.receiptHash);\nprocess.exit(0);\nEOF\nkill %1 2>/dev/null; AGENT_ID=1 PAY_TO=0x89E9E1ab11dD1B138b1dcE6d6A4a0926aaFD5029 RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY=0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d ORIGIN=http://localhost:8403 PORT=8403 FACILITATOR_URL=http://localhost:8500 npx tsx server.testnet.ts > /tmp/srv3.log 2>&1 &\nsleep 1; npx tsx smoke.ts; echo \"--- server log ---\"; cat /tmp/srv3.log"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1182,"cache_read_input_tokens":101812,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1182},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"b53e0781-011f-4bac-9a89-457bc109854f","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:35:25.942Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvNrtsA6z1v6HAgbeTU"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvbhShhPQYVLMzAYX6n","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the design document:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":278,"cache_read_input_tokens":116736,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":278},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"295394f9-b7a6-4ac1-8a15-59d8d402bd59","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:38:06.834Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvbgUQtS182gawiAAHt"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvbhShhPQYVLMzAYX6n","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YXgW4yiDkfzVKZ2GByFqFT","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/design.md","content":"# forecast.example.com — service-side design\n\nWeather forecasts sold to autonomous agents. **$0.35 per call, native USDC on Base**, no\naccounts, no keys, no human anywhere in the loop.\n\nFour standards carry the whole design. Nothing here is bespoke protocol:\n\n| Concern | Standard | Where it lives |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Cold-start discovery + identity | **ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry** (ERC-721) | Base `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` |\n| Third-party-attested ratings | **ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry** | Base `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` |\n| Per-call billing over HTTP | **x402** v2, `exact` scheme | `GET /v1/forecast` → 402 → retry with signature |\n| Paying with USDC and zero ETH | **EIP-3009** `transferWithAuthorization` | USDC `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` |\n\n---\n\n## 1. End-to-end flow, cold client\n\nA client agent that has never heard of this service, holding only USDC:\n\n1. **Find.** The agent scans `Registered(uint256 indexed agentId, string agentURI, address indexed owner)`\n   events on the IdentityRegistry — or receives an `agentId` from any third party, index, or\n   referral. Every route ends at the same canonical record; no catalog operated by this service\n   is involved at any point.\n2. **Read the registration file.** `tokenURI(agentId)` → `ipfs://…` → the ERC-8004\n   registration-v1 JSON: name, description, `services[]` (x402 endpoint, A2A card, agent wallet,\n   receipt signer), `x402Support: true`, `active: true`, `registrations[]`, `supportedTrust`.\n3. **Check the domain is really ours.** The agentURI is on IPFS, so it is served from a\n   different domain than the endpoints. The client fetches\n   `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json` and confirms its\n   `registrations[]` contains `{agentId, agentRegistry: \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169…\"}` matching the\n   onchain token. Without that file, control of `forecast.example.com` is unproven and anyone\n   could advertise our endpoint under their own agentId. **The A2A `agent-card.json` does not do\n   this job** — it is a capability description, served alongside, not a binding.\n4. **Check the money lands where the identity says.** `getAgentWallet(agentId)` must equal the\n   `payTo` that appears in the 402. This closes off an interposed proxy quoting our identity and\n   its own payout address.\n5. **Judge.** `ReputationRegistry.getSummary(agentId, [clients it trusts], tag1, tag2)`, or\n   `readAllFeedback` for the raw rows. All ratings were written by the clients who paid — the\n   registry rejects feedback from the agent's owner and operators, so nothing here is self-issued.\n6. **Price it.** `GET /v1/forecast?lat=…&lon=…` unpaid → `402` with a `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` header:\n   `amount: \"350000\"`, `asset: 0x8335…`, `network: eip155:8453`, `payTo`, plus a JSON body\n   repeating the identity and reputation pointers. The 402 *is* the price list; nothing needs to\n   be read by a human.\n7. **Pay.** The client signs an EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization` offchain (no transaction, no\n   gas) and retries with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE`.\n8. **Get served.** The server verifies through a facilitator, runs the handler, then settles.\n   Response: `200` + forecast JSON + `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` (settlement tx) + a signed receipt + a\n   `feedback` block containing the literal `giveFeedback` arguments to use.\n9. **Rate.** The client calls `ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback(agentId, value, valueDecimals,\n   tag1, tag2, endpoint, feedbackURI, feedbackHash)` on Base. The next cold client reads it at\n   step 5.\n\nThe only inputs a client needs to start are: a chain RPC, an `agentId`, and USDC.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Payment design\n\n**Scheme.** x402 v2, `exact`, network `eip155:8453`, asset transfer method `eip3009`, payment\nflow `authorization` (verify → serve → settle). Chosen deliberately over the alternatives in the\nsame SDK:\n\n- `batch-settlement` / `escrow` would mean holding a client balance. The brief forbids\n  pre-funded balances held by us, and it would reintroduce account state.\n- `upto` is for metered/variable pricing. Ours is a flat $0.35.\n\n**Amount.** `350000`. USDC has 6 decimals; amounts go on the wire in base units. Not `0.35`, not\nan 18-decimal figure.\n\n**Asset.** `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` — native USDC on Base. Two lookalikes are\nwrong here: `0xA0b86991…eB48` is Ethereum mainnet USDC, `0xd9aAEc86…b6CA` is bridged USDbC on\nBase. Only native USDC gives us the EIP-3009 path we depend on.\n\nThe price is pinned as an explicit `AssetAmount` rather than the string `\"$0.35\"`, so the asset\naddress and base-unit amount are stated in code rather than resolved from a default table. That\nmeans we also supply the EIP-712 domain the client needs to sign against USDC:\n`extra: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" }`.\n\n**Why the caller needs no ETH.** USDC implements EIP-3009. The client signs a\n`transferWithAuthorization` authorization offchain; the **facilitator submits it onchain and pays\nthe gas**, and the value moves payer → `payTo` in that one transaction. This is the mechanism —\nnot an ERC-4337 bundler, not an unnamed \"relayer\", not ERC-2612 `permit` followed by\n`transferFrom`. The server never touches gas either: it only speaks HTTP to the facilitator.\n\n**Refund/charge boundary.** The handler runs before settlement. Any 4xx/5xx from the handler\ncancels the verified payment, so bad parameters (`400`) and upstream outages (`502`) are never\ncharged. Verified in testing: an error response carries no `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` header and no\nsettlement occurs. The residual asymmetry is the reverse case — data served, settlement then\nfails (nonce race, reorg, facilitator outage between verify and settle). The facilitator checks\nbalance and authorization validity *before* the handler runs, so this is rare; the alternative\n(settle-before-handler) trades it for charging on failed responses, which is worse for a data\nAPI. `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` reports the failure honestly when it happens.\n\n**Replay.** The EIP-3009 authorization nonce is single-use and enforced by the USDC contract\nitself. No nonce bookkeeping on our side.\n\n**EIP-7702 is not used and is not needed here.** It matters on the *caller's* side — an existing\nEOA gaining batching or session-key auth without changing address — and nothing in this service\nrequires the caller to have it. Callers who do have a 7702 delegation pay through the identical\npath; the facilitator's signature verification handles code-bearing EOAs.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Identity and reputation design\n\n### Registration\n\n`IdentityRegistry.register(string agentURI) returns (uint256 agentId)` mints an ERC-721. **The\n`agentId` is the tokenId the registry assigns** — it is not a UUID, slug, or hash we pick, and it\nis only known after the mint. Clients address us chain-scoped as\n`agentRegistry: eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` + that `agentId`.\n\nThe registration file (the `tokenURI` target) is pinned on IPFS so it survives our servers going\ndown and cannot be silently rewritten. Shape:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"forecast.example.com\",\n  \"description\": \"Per-call weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35 USDC on Base, x402.\",\n  \"image\": \"ipfs://…\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"x402\",          \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/v1/forecast\" },\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\",           \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json\" },\n    { \"name\": \"agentWallet\",   \"endpoint\": \"eip155:8453:0x…payTo\" },\n    { \"name\": \"receiptSigner\", \"endpoint\": \"eip155:8453:0x…signer\" }\n  ],\n  \"registrations\": [\n    { \"agentId\": 42, \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" }\n  ],\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\"]\n}\n```\n\n### Endpoint-domain binding\n\n`https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json`, served by `server.ts`, with a\n`registrations[]` entry matching the onchain agent. This is the step that is routinely skipped and\nwithout it the endpoints are unbound: the agentURI lives on IPFS, so serving it proves nothing\nabout who controls this domain. (If we instead put the agentURI at an `https://` URL on this same\ndomain, control would be proven by that file and this one would be redundant — we rely on the\n`.well-known` file, and say so, rather than leaving it ambiguous.)\n\n### Ratings\n\nClient-attested only. After a paid call the caller calls, on Base:\n\n```\ngiveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals,\n             string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint,\n             string feedbackURI, bytes32 feedbackHash)\n```\n\nThe 200 response hands the caller every argument pre-filled except the score itself:\n`agentId`, `endpoint` (`\"GET https://forecast.example.com/v1/forecast\"`), suggested tags\n(`forecast-quality`, `latency`), `feedbackURI` (the receipt URL) and `feedbackHash` (sha256 of the\ncanonical receipt core). Values are fixed point: a score of 87/100 is `value=87, valueDecimals=0`;\n99.77% would be `value=9977, valueDecimals=2`.\n\nReceipts make a rating checkable by third parties: the receipt core is immutable, hashed, and\nsigned by a dedicated receipt-signing key whose address is announced both in the registration file\nand onchain via `setMetadata(agentId, \"receiptSigner\", …)`. The settlement tx hash is stored\n*outside* the hashed core, so the caller can compute `feedbackHash` the instant it receives the\nresponse, before the settlement transaction is mined. A rater can also be cross-checked with no\nhelp from us at all: their address appears in the onchain feedback and in the USDC transfer to\n`payTo`.\n\nWe cannot rate ourselves — the registry rejects feedback from the agent's owner and operators. We\ncan only reply, via `appendResponse(agentId, clientAddress, feedbackIndex, responseURI,\nresponseHash)`. Clients can withdraw a rating with `revokeFeedback`.\n\n**Reading is deliberately filtered.** `getSummary` *reverts* with `clientAddresses required` on an\nempty list — the registry itself refuses to hand out an unfiltered average, because unfiltered\ntotals are Sybil bait. `readAllFeedback` accepts an empty list and returns raw rows; our\n`/reputation` mirror uses it only with an explicit warning, and is labelled non-authoritative.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Onchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Address / detail | Used for | Who pays gas |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Base mainnet (`eip155:8453`) | chainId 8453 | settlement + registries | — |\n| Native USDC | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`, 6 decimals, EIP-712 domain `(\"USD Coin\",\"2\")` | the $0.35 | facilitator |\n| ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` (ERC-1967 proxy → impl `0x7274e874CA62410a93Bd8bf61c69d8045E399c02`) | `register`, `tokenURI`, `getAgentWallet`, `setAgentWallet`, `setMetadata` | us, one-time at bootstrap |\n| ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` (proxy → impl `0x16e0FA7f7C56B9a767E34B192B51f921BE31dA34`) | `giveFeedback`, `getSummary`, `readAllFeedback`, `appendResponse` | the rating client |\n| Base RPC | any provider | `/reputation` mirror only; the service serves 402s and forecasts without it | — |\n\nAddresses and ABIs above were read from Base mainnet, not from memory. Two notes carried from\nthat check:\n\n- The **testnet registries are a different pair** (`0x8004A818BFB912233c491871b3d84c89A494BD9e` /\n  `0x8004B663056A597Dffe9eCcC1965A193B7388713`). The mainnet addresses have no code on Base\n  Sepolia, so a staging deployment must switch both the registry addresses and the USDC address.\n- Both registries are **ERC-1967 proxies** and the ERC-8004 EIP is still **Draft**. Pin the\n  implementation addresses in monitoring and watch `Upgraded` events; treat any signature not\n  re-verified onchain as movable.\n\n## 5. Offchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Role | Failure mode | Mitigation |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| **x402 facilitator** | verify + settle, submits the EIP-3009 authorization and pays gas | no settlement → every call 402s | `x402ResourceServer` accepts an array of facilitator clients; run a self-hosted facilitator as fallback |\n| **Coinbase CDP credentials** | auth for the public Coinbase facilitator (`@coinbase/x402`) | 401 on `/supported` at boot | keys in env; a self-hosted facilitator (`FACILITATOR_URL`) needs no CDP account but does need a gas-funded signer |\n| **IPFS pinning** | hosts the registration file behind `agentURI` | agentURI unresolvable → clients cannot evaluate us | pinning service + our own node; optionally mirror the same JSON on this domain |\n| **Receipt storage** | serves `feedbackURI` targets referenced by onchain ratings | dangling feedback URIs | `RECEIPT_DIR` for durability; production should use S3/IPFS. `feedbackHash` stays verifiable regardless |\n| **Upstream forecast provider** (Open-Meteo) | the actual data | `502`, payment cancelled, no revenue | second provider behind the same handler |\n| **DNS + TLS for forecast.example.com** | serves the `.well-known` binding | binding unverifiable → cold clients should refuse to pay | standard availability engineering; the binding file is static and cacheable |\n| **Receipt signing key** | signs receipt cores | forged receipts if leaked | separate hot key holding no funds, rotatable via `setMetadata`; it is not the payout wallet |\n\nNote what is *not* a dependency: no database of users, no API-key store, no invoicing system, no\nbalance ledger, no node of our own, and no catalog we operate. The x402 Bazaar and any other\nindex may list us, but discovery does not depend on them — the registry does that.\n\n---\n\n## 6. What must be published, and where\n\n| Artifact | Location | Mutable? | Purpose |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Registration file (`registration-v1`) | IPFS, CID referenced by `agentURI` onchain | new CID + `setAgentURI` | the record a cold client reads after finding the agentId |\n| `agent-registration.json` | `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json` | yes (served by `server.ts`) | **binds this domain to the onchain agentId** |\n| `agent-card.json` | `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json` | yes | A2A capability + price description. Not a trust anchor |\n| Receipts | `https://forecast.example.com/receipts/{id}` | core immutable; settlement status appended | targets of `feedbackURI` in onchain ratings |\n| Agent image | IPFS | — | referenced by the registration file |\n\n## 7. What must be registered before the service can be found\n\nOrdered; steps 1–6 are one-time and can all be executed by an operator agent holding a key — no\nhuman step, though someone must fund the operator EOA with a small amount of ETH on Base for\nthree transactions.\n\n1. Fund the operator EOA on Base (gas only) and generate two keys: the **payout wallet** and the\n   **receipt signer**.\n2. Pin the registration file to IPFS → `CID₀`.\n3. `IdentityRegistry.register(\"ipfs://CID₀\")` → the registry assigns `agentId`.\n4. The registration file's `registrations[]` can only be filled in now, since `agentId` did not\n   exist before step 3: re-pin with `{agentId, agentRegistry}` → `CID₁`, then\n   `setAgentURI(agentId, \"ipfs://CID₁\")`. (Registering with `\"agentId\": null` and relying on the\n   registry entry alone is an accepted convention seen in live registrations, but the explicit\n   value is better: it makes the file self-describing.)\n5. `setAgentWallet(agentId, payTo, deadline, signature)` — signed by the payout wallet — so the\n   402's `payTo` is verifiable against the onchain identity.\n6. `setMetadata(agentId, \"receiptSigner\", abi.encode(signerAddress))` so receipt signatures are\n   checkable against an onchain-announced key.\n7. Deploy `server.ts` with `AGENT_ID`, `PAY_TO`, `AGENT_URI`, `RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY`, facilitator\n   credentials, and TLS on `forecast.example.com`. The `.well-known` binding goes live with it.\n8. *Optional and non-load-bearing:* announce in the x402 Bazaar or any third-party index. If every\n   index disappears, `Registered` events on Base still lead a client to us.\n\nFacilitator note: the public testnet facilitators do **not** support `eip155:8453` — checking\n`/supported` on `facilitator.x402.rs` returns testnets only. Base-mainnet settlement means the\nCoinbase CDP facilitator (API key required) or a self-hosted facilitator with a gas-funded signer.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Endpoints\n\n| Method | Path | Price | Notes |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| GET | `/v1/forecast?lat&lon&hours` | **$0.35** | 402 → pay → 200 + forecast + receipt + feedback instructions |\n| GET | `/.well-known/agent-registration.json` | free | ERC-8004 domain binding |\n| GET | `/.well-known/agent-card.json` | free | A2A card |\n| GET | `/receipts/:id` | free | signed receipt; `feedbackURI` target |\n| GET | `/reputation[?clients=…&tag1=…]` | free | non-authoritative mirror of the onchain registry |\n| GET | `/health` | free | liveness |\n\nOnly `GET /v1/forecast` is registered with the payment middleware; everything else falls through\nunpriced, so discovery and rating never cost anything.\n\n## 9. How each constraint is met\n\n- **Discovery without a human and without our own catalog** — ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry on Base;\n  the registration file and the `.well-known` binding are machine-readable; the 402 states the\n  price. §1, §3.\n- **Per call, no accounts/keys/subscriptions/invoices/balances** — x402 `exact` with the\n  `authorization` flow: each HTTP call carries its own payment, settled payer → `payTo`. No state\n  survives a request except a receipt. §2.\n- **USDC but no ETH** — EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization`, submitted and gas-paid by the\n  facilitator. §2.\n- **Ratings other agents can act on** — ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry, written by the paying client,\n  readable by anyone before choosing us, unforgeable by us. §3.\n- **No human in the flow** — every step above is an RPC call, an HTTP call, or a signature.\n  Bootstrap (§7) is one-time and scriptable; the only human-shaped act left is funding the\n  operator EOA with gas once.\n\n## 10. Known risks\n\n- ERC-8004 is a Draft EIP behind upgradeable proxies; a registry upgrade could change behaviour\n  under us. Monitor `Upgraded`.\n- Reputation is only as good as the client filter. Callers who read unfiltered totals will be\n  Sybil-farmed by *some* agent eventually; we surface the warning but cannot enforce it.\n- Facilitator centralisation: while the CDP facilitator is the only one supporting Base mainnet\n  for us, it is a single point of failure for revenue. Self-hosting is the exit.\n- Receipt durability is our obligation for as long as ratings referencing them stay onchain.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":278,"cache_read_input_tokens":116736,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":278},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"e838738f-8334-439e-85cd-67788a4fef2b","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:39:47.894Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvbgUQtS182gawiAAHt"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YXgW4yiDkfzVKZ2GByFqFT","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/design.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"cc233bb0-3f75-4f8c-94e9-46fd735297f7","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:39:47.904Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/design.md","content":"# forecast.example.com — service-side design\n\nWeather forecasts sold to autonomous agents. **$0.35 per call, native USDC on Base**, no\naccounts, no keys, no human anywhere in the loop.\n\nFour standards carry the whole design. Nothing here is bespoke protocol:\n\n| Concern | Standard | Where it lives |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Cold-start discovery + identity | **ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry** (ERC-721) | Base `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` |\n| Third-party-attested ratings | **ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry** | Base `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` |\n| Per-call billing over HTTP | **x402** v2, `exact` scheme | `GET /v1/forecast` → 402 → retry with signature |\n| Paying with USDC and zero ETH | **EIP-3009** `transferWithAuthorization` | USDC `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` |\n\n---\n\n## 1. End-to-end flow, cold client\n\nA client agent that has never heard of this service, holding only USDC:\n\n1. **Find.** The agent scans `Registered(uint256 indexed agentId, string agentURI, address indexed owner)`\n   events on the IdentityRegistry — or receives an `agentId` from any third party, index, or\n   referral. Every route ends at the same canonical record; no catalog operated by this service\n   is involved at any point.\n2. **Read the registration file.** `tokenURI(agentId)` → `ipfs://…` → the ERC-8004\n   registration-v1 JSON: name, description, `services[]` (x402 endpoint, A2A card, agent wallet,\n   receipt signer), `x402Support: true`, `active: true`, `registrations[]`, `supportedTrust`.\n3. **Check the domain is really ours.** The agentURI is on IPFS, so it is served from a\n   different domain than the endpoints. The client fetches\n   `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json` and confirms its\n   `registrations[]` contains `{agentId, agentRegistry: \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169…\"}` matching the\n   onchain token. Without that file, control of `forecast.example.com` is unproven and anyone\n   could advertise our endpoint under their own agentId. **The A2A `agent-card.json` does not do\n   this job** — it is a capability description, served alongside, not a binding.\n4. **Check the money lands where the identity says.** `getAgentWallet(agentId)` must equal the\n   `payTo` that appears in the 402. This closes off an interposed proxy quoting our identity and\n   its own payout address.\n5. **Judge.** `ReputationRegistry.getSummary(agentId, [clients it trusts], tag1, tag2)`, or\n   `readAllFeedback` for the raw rows. All ratings were written by the clients who paid — the\n   registry rejects feedback from the agent's owner and operators, so nothing here is self-issued.\n6. **Price it.** `GET /v1/forecast?lat=…&lon=…` unpaid → `402` with a `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` header:\n   `amount: \"350000\"`, `asset: 0x8335…`, `network: eip155:8453`, `payTo`, plus a JSON body\n   repeating the identity and reputation pointers. The 402 *is* the price list; nothing needs to\n   be read by a human.\n7. **Pay.** The client signs an EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization` offchain (no transaction, no\n   gas) and retries with `PAYMENT-SIGNATURE`.\n8. **Get served.** The server verifies through a facilitator, runs the handler, then settles.\n   Response: `200` + forecast JSON + `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` (settlement tx) + a signed receipt + a\n   `feedback` block containing the literal `giveFeedback` arguments to use.\n9. **Rate.** The client calls `ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback(agentId, value, valueDecimals,\n   tag1, tag2, endpoint, feedbackURI, feedbackHash)` on Base. The next cold client reads it at\n   step 5.\n\nThe only inputs a client needs to start are: a chain RPC, an `agentId`, and USDC.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Payment design\n\n**Scheme.** x402 v2, `exact`, network `eip155:8453`, asset transfer method `eip3009`, payment\nflow `authorization` (verify → serve → settle). Chosen deliberately over the alternatives in the\nsame SDK:\n\n- `batch-settlement` / `escrow` would mean holding a client balance. The brief forbids\n  pre-funded balances held by us, and it would reintroduce account state.\n- `upto` is for metered/variable pricing. Ours is a flat $0.35.\n\n**Amount.** `350000`. USDC has 6 decimals; amounts go on the wire in base units. Not `0.35`, not\nan 18-decimal figure.\n\n**Asset.** `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` — native USDC on Base. Two lookalikes are\nwrong here: `0xA0b86991…eB48` is Ethereum mainnet USDC, `0xd9aAEc86…b6CA` is bridged USDbC on\nBase. Only native USDC gives us the EIP-3009 path we depend on.\n\nThe price is pinned as an explicit `AssetAmount` rather than the string `\"$0.35\"`, so the asset\naddress and base-unit amount are stated in code rather than resolved from a default table. That\nmeans we also supply the EIP-712 domain the client needs to sign against USDC:\n`extra: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" }`.\n\n**Why the caller needs no ETH.** USDC implements EIP-3009. The client signs a\n`transferWithAuthorization` authorization offchain; the **facilitator submits it onchain and pays\nthe gas**, and the value moves payer → `payTo` in that one transaction. This is the mechanism —\nnot an ERC-4337 bundler, not an unnamed \"relayer\", not ERC-2612 `permit` followed by\n`transferFrom`. The server never touches gas either: it only speaks HTTP to the facilitator.\n\n**Refund/charge boundary.** The handler runs before settlement. Any 4xx/5xx from the handler\ncancels the verified payment, so bad parameters (`400`) and upstream outages (`502`) are never\ncharged. Verified in testing: an error response carries no `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` header and no\nsettlement occurs. The residual asymmetry is the reverse case — data served, settlement then\nfails (nonce race, reorg, facilitator outage between verify and settle). The facilitator checks\nbalance and authorization validity *before* the handler runs, so this is rare; the alternative\n(settle-before-handler) trades it for charging on failed responses, which is worse for a data\nAPI. `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` reports the failure honestly when it happens.\n\n**Replay.** The EIP-3009 authorization nonce is single-use and enforced by the USDC contract\nitself. No nonce bookkeeping on our side.\n\n**EIP-7702 is not used and is not needed here.** It matters on the *caller's* side — an existing\nEOA gaining batching or session-key auth without changing address — and nothing in this service\nrequires the caller to have it. Callers who do have a 7702 delegation pay through the identical\npath; the facilitator's signature verification handles code-bearing EOAs.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Identity and reputation design\n\n### Registration\n\n`IdentityRegistry.register(string agentURI) returns (uint256 agentId)` mints an ERC-721. **The\n`agentId` is the tokenId the registry assigns** — it is not a UUID, slug, or hash we pick, and it\nis only known after the mint. Clients address us chain-scoped as\n`agentRegistry: eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` + that `agentId`.\n\nThe registration file (the `tokenURI` target) is pinned on IPFS so it survives our servers going\ndown and cannot be silently rewritten. Shape:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"forecast.example.com\",\n  \"description\": \"Per-call weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35 USDC on Base, x402.\",\n  \"image\": \"ipfs://…\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"x402\",          \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/v1/forecast\" },\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\",           \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json\" },\n    { \"name\": \"agentWallet\",   \"endpoint\": \"eip155:8453:0x…payTo\" },\n    { \"name\": \"receiptSigner\", \"endpoint\": \"eip155:8453:0x…signer\" }\n  ],\n  \"registrations\": [\n    { \"agentId\": 42, \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" }\n  ],\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\"]\n}\n```\n\n### Endpoint-domain binding\n\n`https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json`, served by `server.ts`, with a\n`registrations[]` entry matching the onchain agent. This is the step that is routinely skipped and\nwithout it the endpoints are unbound: the agentURI lives on IPFS, so serving it proves nothing\nabout who controls this domain. (If we instead put the agentURI at an `https://` URL on this same\ndomain, control would be proven by that file and this one would be redundant — we rely on the\n`.well-known` file, and say so, rather than leaving it ambiguous.)\n\n### Ratings\n\nClient-attested only. After a paid call the caller calls, on Base:\n\n```\ngiveFeedback(uint256 agentId, int128 value, uint8 valueDecimals,\n             string tag1, string tag2, string endpoint,\n             string feedbackURI, bytes32 feedbackHash)\n```\n\nThe 200 response hands the caller every argument pre-filled except the score itself:\n`agentId`, `endpoint` (`\"GET https://forecast.example.com/v1/forecast\"`), suggested tags\n(`forecast-quality`, `latency`), `feedbackURI` (the receipt URL) and `feedbackHash` (sha256 of the\ncanonical receipt core). Values are fixed point: a score of 87/100 is `value=87, valueDecimals=0`;\n99.77% would be `value=9977, valueDecimals=2`.\n\nReceipts make a rating checkable by third parties: the receipt core is immutable, hashed, and\nsigned by a dedicated receipt-signing key whose address is announced both in the registration file\nand onchain via `setMetadata(agentId, \"receiptSigner\", …)`. The settlement tx hash is stored\n*outside* the hashed core, so the caller can compute `feedbackHash` the instant it receives the\nresponse, before the settlement transaction is mined. A rater can also be cross-checked with no\nhelp from us at all: their address appears in the onchain feedback and in the USDC transfer to\n`payTo`.\n\nWe cannot rate ourselves — the registry rejects feedback from the agent's owner and operators. We\ncan only reply, via `appendResponse(agentId, clientAddress, feedbackIndex, responseURI,\nresponseHash)`. Clients can withdraw a rating with `revokeFeedback`.\n\n**Reading is deliberately filtered.** `getSummary` *reverts* with `clientAddresses required` on an\nempty list — the registry itself refuses to hand out an unfiltered average, because unfiltered\ntotals are Sybil bait. `readAllFeedback` accepts an empty list and returns raw rows; our\n`/reputation` mirror uses it only with an explicit warning, and is labelled non-authoritative.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Onchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Address / detail | Used for | Who pays gas |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Base mainnet (`eip155:8453`) | chainId 8453 | settlement + registries | — |\n| Native USDC | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`, 6 decimals, EIP-712 domain `(\"USD Coin\",\"2\")` | the $0.35 | facilitator |\n| ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` (ERC-1967 proxy → impl `0x7274e874CA62410a93Bd8bf61c69d8045E399c02`) | `register`, `tokenURI`, `getAgentWallet`, `setAgentWallet`, `setMetadata` | us, one-time at bootstrap |\n| ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` (proxy → impl `0x16e0FA7f7C56B9a767E34B192B51f921BE31dA34`) | `giveFeedback`, `getSummary`, `readAllFeedback`, `appendResponse` | the rating client |\n| Base RPC | any provider | `/reputation` mirror only; the service serves 402s and forecasts without it | — |\n\nAddresses and ABIs above were read from Base mainnet, not from memory. Two notes carried from\nthat check:\n\n- The **testnet registries are a different pair** (`0x8004A818BFB912233c491871b3d84c89A494BD9e` /\n  `0x8004B663056A597Dffe9eCcC1965A193B7388713`). The mainnet addresses have no code on Base\n  Sepolia, so a staging deployment must switch both the registry addresses and the USDC address.\n- Both registries are **ERC-1967 proxies** and the ERC-8004 EIP is still **Draft**. Pin the\n  implementation addresses in monitoring and watch `Upgraded` events; treat any signature not\n  re-verified onchain as movable.\n\n## 5. Offchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Role | Failure mode | Mitigation |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| **x402 facilitator** | verify + settle, submits the EIP-3009 authorization and pays gas | no settlement → every call 402s | `x402ResourceServer` accepts an array of facilitator clients; run a self-hosted facilitator as fallback |\n| **Coinbase CDP credentials** | auth for the public Coinbase facilitator (`@coinbase/x402`) | 401 on `/supported` at boot | keys in env; a self-hosted facilitator (`FACILITATOR_URL`) needs no CDP account but does need a gas-funded signer |\n| **IPFS pinning** | hosts the registration file behind `agentURI` | agentURI unresolvable → clients cannot evaluate us | pinning service + our own node; optionally mirror the same JSON on this domain |\n| **Receipt storage** | serves `feedbackURI` targets referenced by onchain ratings | dangling feedback URIs | `RECEIPT_DIR` for durability; production should use S3/IPFS. `feedbackHash` stays verifiable regardless |\n| **Upstream forecast provider** (Open-Meteo) | the actual data | `502`, payment cancelled, no revenue | second provider behind the same handler |\n| **DNS + TLS for forecast.example.com** | serves the `.well-known` binding | binding unverifiable → cold clients should refuse to pay | standard availability engineering; the binding file is static and cacheable |\n| **Receipt signing key** | signs receipt cores | forged receipts if leaked | separate hot key holding no funds, rotatable via `setMetadata`; it is not the payout wallet |\n\nNote what is *not* a dependency: no database of users, no API-key store, no invoicing system, no\nbalance ledger, no node of our own, and no catalog we operate. The x402 Bazaar and any other\nindex may list us, but discovery does not depend on them — the registry does that.\n\n---\n\n## 6. What must be published, and where\n\n| Artifact | Location | Mutable? | Purpose |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Registration file (`registration-v1`) | IPFS, CID referenced by `agentURI` onchain | new CID + `setAgentURI` | the record a cold client reads after finding the agentId |\n| `agent-registration.json` | `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json` | yes (served by `server.ts`) | **binds this domain to the onchain agentId** |\n| `agent-card.json` | `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json` | yes | A2A capability + price description. Not a trust anchor |\n| Receipts | `https://forecast.example.com/receipts/{id}` | core immutable; settlement status appended | targets of `feedbackURI` in onchain ratings |\n| Agent image | IPFS | — | referenced by the registration file |\n\n## 7. What must be registered before the service can be found\n\nOrdered; steps 1–6 are one-time and can all be executed by an operator agent holding a key — no\nhuman step, though someone must fund the operator EOA with a small amount of ETH on Base for\nthree transactions.\n\n1. Fund the operator EOA on Base (gas only) and generate two keys: the **payout wallet** and the\n   **receipt signer**.\n2. Pin the registration file to IPFS → `CID₀`.\n3. `IdentityRegistry.register(\"ipfs://CID₀\")` → the registry assigns `agentId`.\n4. The registration file's `registrations[]` can only be filled in now, since `agentId` did not\n   exist before step 3: re-pin with `{agentId, agentRegistry}` → `CID₁`, then\n   `setAgentURI(agentId, \"ipfs://CID₁\")`. (Registering with `\"agentId\": null` and relying on the\n   registry entry alone is an accepted convention seen in live registrations, but the explicit\n   value is better: it makes the file self-describing.)\n5. `setAgentWallet(agentId, payTo, deadline, signature)` — signed by the payout wallet — so the\n   402's `payTo` is verifiable against the onchain identity.\n6. `setMetadata(agentId, \"receiptSigner\", abi.encode(signerAddress))` so receipt signatures are\n   checkable against an onchain-announced key.\n7. Deploy `server.ts` with `AGENT_ID`, `PAY_TO`, `AGENT_URI`, `RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY`, facilitator\n   credentials, and TLS on `forecast.example.com`. The `.well-known` binding goes live with it.\n8. *Optional and non-load-bearing:* announce in the x402 Bazaar or any third-party index. If every\n   index disappears, `Registered` events on Base still lead a client to us.\n\nFacilitator note: the public testnet facilitators do **not** support `eip155:8453` — checking\n`/supported` on `facilitator.x402.rs` returns testnets only. Base-mainnet settlement means the\nCoinbase CDP facilitator (API key required) or a self-hosted facilitator with a gas-funded signer.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Endpoints\n\n| Method | Path | Price | Notes |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| GET | `/v1/forecast?lat&lon&hours` | **$0.35** | 402 → pay → 200 + forecast + receipt + feedback instructions |\n| GET | `/.well-known/agent-registration.json` | free | ERC-8004 domain binding |\n| GET | `/.well-known/agent-card.json` | free | A2A card |\n| GET | `/receipts/:id` | free | signed receipt; `feedbackURI` target |\n| GET | `/reputation[?clients=…&tag1=…]` | free | non-authoritative mirror of the onchain registry |\n| GET | `/health` | free | liveness |\n\nOnly `GET /v1/forecast` is registered with the payment middleware; everything else falls through\nunpriced, so discovery and rating never cost anything.\n\n## 9. How each constraint is met\n\n- **Discovery without a human and without our own catalog** — ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry on Base;\n  the registration file and the `.well-known` binding are machine-readable; the 402 states the\n  price. §1, §3.\n- **Per call, no accounts/keys/subscriptions/invoices/balances** — x402 `exact` with the\n  `authorization` flow: each HTTP call carries its own payment, settled payer → `payTo`. No state\n  survives a request except a receipt. §2.\n- **USDC but no ETH** — EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization`, submitted and gas-paid by the\n  facilitator. §2.\n- **Ratings other agents can act on** — ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry, written by the paying client,\n  readable by anyone before choosing us, unforgeable by us. §3.\n- **No human in the flow** — every step above is an RPC call, an HTTP call, or a signature.\n  Bootstrap (§7) is one-time and scriptable; the only human-shaped act left is funding the\n  operator EOA with gas once.\n\n## 10. Known risks\n\n- ERC-8004 is a Draft EIP behind upgradeable proxies; a registry upgrade could change behaviour\n  under us. Monitor `Upgraded`.\n- Reputation is only as good as the client filter. Callers who read unfiltered totals will be\n  Sybil-farmed by *some* agent eventually; we surface the warning but cannot enforce it.\n- Facilitator centralisation: while the CDP facilitator is the only one supporting Base mainnet\n  for us, it is a single point of failure for revenue. Self-hosting is the exit.\n- Receipt durability is our obligation for as long as ratings referencing them stay onchain.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvkbq7iPb659ztAyiTi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019TtYnJfGP4u3BqLAB7UfWp","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"W=/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace; cd $W && node -e \"\nconst {createPublicClient,http,parseAbi}=require('viem');const {base}=require('viem/chains');\nconst c=createPublicClient({chain:base,transport:http('https://mainnet.base.org')});\nc.readContract({address:'0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432',abi:parseAbi(['function eip712Domain() view returns (bytes1,string,string,uint256,address,bytes32,uint256[])']),functionName:'eip712Domain'}).then(r=>console.log(r.slice(0,5)));\n\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":846,"cache_read_input_tokens":127991,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":846},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"58d30a6b-d909-4cc8-9b06-0761108b62aa","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:40:14.533Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvkaorF2yVBMjmMcR8W"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019TtYnJfGP4u3BqLAB7UfWp","type":"tool_result","content":"[\n  '0x0f',\n  'ERC8004IdentityRegistry',\n  '1',\n  8453n,\n  '0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432'\n]","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"173115d1-d7c4-4e33-b0cd-2350fcdac843","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:40:15.371Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"[\n  '0x0f',\n  'ERC8004IdentityRegistry',\n  '1',\n  8453n,\n  '0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432'\n]","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeRvmHyMDBs8yvRYM5o8i","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013J6x677xqEruapJN7czUTV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/register-agent.ts","content":"/**\n * One-time bootstrap: mint the ERC-8004 identity this service is discovered by.\n *\n * Implements design.md §7 steps 3-6. Run once, from an operator EOA holding a\n * little ETH on Base for gas; nothing here needs a human beyond that funding.\n *\n *   OPERATOR_KEY=0x…  PAY_TO_KEY=0x…  AGENT_URI=ipfs://CID0 \\\n *   RECEIPT_SIGNER_ADDRESS=0x…  npx tsx register-agent.ts\n *\n * Prints the assigned agentId. Feed it back into the registration file\n * (`registrations[]`), re-pin, and call setAgentURI with the new CID — the\n * agentId cannot be known before the mint, which is why this is two passes.\n */\n\nimport { createPublicClient, createWalletClient, http, parseAbi, getAddress, encodePacked } from \"viem\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\n\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" as const;\nconst RPC_URL = process.env.BASE_RPC_URL ?? \"https://mainnet.base.org\";\n\nconst identityAbi = parseAbi([\n  \"function register(string agentURI) returns (uint256 agentId)\",\n  \"function setAgentURI(uint256 agentId, string newURI)\",\n  \"function setAgentWallet(uint256 agentId, address newWallet, uint256 deadline, bytes signature)\",\n  \"function setMetadata(uint256 agentId, string metadataKey, bytes metadataValue)\",\n  \"function getAgentWallet(uint256 agentId) view returns (address)\",\n  \"function tokenURI(uint256 tokenId) view returns (string)\",\n  \"event Registered(uint256 indexed agentId, string agentURI, address indexed owner)\",\n]);\n\nfunction required(name: string): string {\n  const value = process.env[name];\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`missing required env var ${name}`);\n  return value;\n}\n\nconst operator = privateKeyToAccount(required(\"OPERATOR_KEY\") as `0x${string}`);\n/** The payout wallet must sign its own binding; it never needs gas. */\nconst payoutWallet = privateKeyToAccount(required(\"PAY_TO_KEY\") as `0x${string}`);\nconst agentURI = required(\"AGENT_URI\");\nconst receiptSigner = getAddress(required(\"RECEIPT_SIGNER_ADDRESS\"));\n\nconst publicClient = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\nconst wallet = createWalletClient({ account: operator, chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\n\n// --- 3. Mint. The registry assigns the agentId; we do not choose it. ---------\nconst registerHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"register\",\n  args: [agentURI],\n});\nconst receipt = await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: registerHash });\n\nconst registered = receipt.logs\n  .map((log) => {\n    try {\n      return publicClient.chain?.id && log.address.toLowerCase() === IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()\n        ? log\n        : undefined;\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  })\n  .filter(Boolean);\nif (registered.length === 0) throw new Error(\"register() emitted no logs from the registry\");\n\nconst { decodeEventLog } = await import(\"viem\");\nlet agentId: bigint | undefined;\nfor (const log of registered) {\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log!.data, topics: log!.topics });\n    if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;\n  } catch {\n    /* other events from the same contract */\n  }\n}\nif (agentId === undefined) throw new Error(\"could not read agentId from Registered event\");\nconsole.log(`agentId = ${agentId}  (tx ${registerHash})`);\n\n// --- 5. Bind the payout wallet ----------------------------------------------\n// register() defaults agentWallet to the operator EOA. Point it at the address\n// that appears as `payTo` in the 402, so clients can verify the two match.\n// EIP-712 domain and typehash read from the deployed implementation; the\n// contract enforces deadline <= now + 5 minutes.\nconst deadline = BigInt(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 240);\nconst walletSignature = await payoutWallet.signTypedData({\n  domain: {\n    name: \"ERC8004IdentityRegistry\",\n    version: \"1\",\n    chainId: base.id,\n    verifyingContract: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  },\n  types: {\n    AgentWalletSet: [\n      { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n      { name: \"newWallet\", type: \"address\" },\n      { name: \"owner\", type: \"address\" },\n      { name: \"deadline\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    ],\n  },\n  primaryType: \"AgentWalletSet\",\n  message: {\n    agentId,\n    newWallet: payoutWallet.address,\n    owner: operator.address,\n    deadline,\n  },\n});\n\nconst walletHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"setAgentWallet\",\n  args: [agentId, payoutWallet.address, deadline, walletSignature],\n});\nawait publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: walletHash });\nconsole.log(`agentWallet = ${payoutWallet.address}  (tx ${walletHash})`);\n\n// --- 6. Announce the receipt signer -----------------------------------------\n// \"agentWallet\" is a reserved metadata key, so this uses its own key.\nconst metadataHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"setMetadata\",\n  args: [agentId, \"receiptSigner\", encodePacked([\"address\"], [receiptSigner])],\n});\nawait publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: metadataHash });\nconsole.log(`receiptSigner = ${receiptSigner}  (tx ${metadataHash})`);\n\nconsole.log(`\nNext:\n  1. Add {\"agentId\": ${agentId}, \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:${base.id}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}\"}\n     to registrations[] in the registration file, re-pin to IPFS -> CID1.\n  2. setAgentURI(${agentId}, \"ipfs://CID1\").\n  3. Deploy server.ts with AGENT_ID=${agentId} PAY_TO=${payoutWallet.address}.\n     Its /.well-known/agent-registration.json binds this domain to the agentId.`);\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":703,"cache_read_input_tokens":128837,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":703},"output_tokens":17,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"83b77e5f-0feb-4c44-b1a6-6a4c33f910c5","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:40:40.481Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvmH3YRw1p4dj1LVWS1"}

Run once, from an operator EOA holding a\n * little ETH on Base for gas; nothing here needs a human beyond that funding.\n *\n *   OPERATOR_KEY=0x…  PAY_TO_KEY=0x…  AGENT_URI=ipfs://CID0 \\\n *   RECEIPT_SIGNER_ADDRESS=0x…  npx tsx register-agent.ts\n *\n * Prints the assigned agentId. Feed it back into the registration file\n * (`registrations[]`), re-pin, and call setAgentURI with the new CID — the\n * agentId cannot be known before the mint, which is why this is two passes.\n */\n\nimport { createPublicClient, createWalletClient, http, parseAbi, getAddress, encodePacked } from \"viem\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\n\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" as const;\nconst RPC_URL = process.env.BASE_RPC_URL ?? \"https://mainnet.base.org\";\n\nconst identityAbi = parseAbi([\n  \"function register(string agentURI) returns (uint256 agentId)\",\n  \"function setAgentURI(uint256 agentId, string newURI)\",\n  \"function setAgentWallet(uint256 agentId, address newWallet, uint256 deadline, bytes signature)\",\n  \"function setMetadata(uint256 agentId, string metadataKey, bytes metadataValue)\",\n  \"function getAgentWallet(uint256 agentId) view returns (address)\",\n  \"function tokenURI(uint256 tokenId) view returns (string)\",\n  \"event Registered(uint256 indexed agentId, string agentURI, address indexed owner)\",\n]);\n\nfunction required(name: string): string {\n  const value = process.env[name];\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`missing required env var ${name}`);\n  return value;\n}\n\nconst operator = privateKeyToAccount(required(\"OPERATOR_KEY\") as `0x${string}`);\n/** The payout wallet must sign its own binding; it never needs gas. */\nconst payoutWallet = privateKeyToAccount(required(\"PAY_TO_KEY\") as `0x${string}`);\nconst agentURI = required(\"AGENT_URI\");\nconst receiptSigner = getAddress(required(\"RECEIPT_SIGNER_ADDRESS\"));\n\nconst publicClient = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\nconst wallet = createWalletClient({ account: operator, chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\n\n// --- 3. Mint. The registry assigns the agentId; we do not choose it. ---------\nconst registerHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"register\",\n  args: [agentURI],\n});\nconst receipt = await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: registerHash });\n\nconst registered = receipt.logs\n  .map((log) => {\n    try {\n      return publicClient.chain?.id && log.address.toLowerCase() === IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()\n        ? log\n        : undefined;\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  })\n  .filter(Boolean);\nif (registered.length === 0) throw new Error(\"register() emitted no logs from the registry\");\n\nconst { decodeEventLog } = await import(\"viem\");\nlet agentId: bigint | undefined;\nfor (const log of registered) {\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log!.data, topics: log!.topics });\n    if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;\n  } catch {\n    /* other events from the same contract */\n  }\n}\nif (agentId === undefined) throw new Error(\"could not read agentId from Registered event\");\nconsole.log(`agentId = ${agentId}  (tx ${registerHash})`);\n\n// --- 5. Bind the payout wallet ----------------------------------------------\n// register() defaults agentWallet to the operator EOA. Point it at the address\n// that appears as `payTo` in the 402, so clients can verify the two match.\n// EIP-712 domain and typehash read from the deployed implementation; the\n// contract enforces deadline <= now + 5 minutes.\nconst deadline = BigInt(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 240);\nconst walletSignature = await payoutWallet.signTypedData({\n  domain: {\n    name: \"ERC8004IdentityRegistry\",\n    version: \"1\",\n    chainId: base.id,\n    verifyingContract: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  },\n  types: {\n    AgentWalletSet: [\n      { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n      { name: \"newWallet\", type: \"address\" },\n      { name: \"owner\", type: \"address\" },\n      { name: \"deadline\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    ],\n  },\n  primaryType: \"AgentWalletSet\",\n  message: {\n    agentId,\n    newWallet: payoutWallet.address,\n    owner: operator.address,\n    deadline,\n  },\n});\n\nconst walletHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"setAgentWallet\",\n  args: [agentId, payoutWallet.address, deadline, walletSignature],\n});\nawait publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: walletHash });\nconsole.log(`agentWallet = ${payoutWallet.address}  (tx ${walletHash})`);\n\n// --- 6. Announce the receipt signer -----------------------------------------\n// \"agentWallet\" is a reserved metadata key, so this uses its own key.\nconst metadataHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"setMetadata\",\n  args: [agentId, \"receiptSigner\", encodePacked([\"address\"], [receiptSigner])],\n});\nawait publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: metadataHash });\nconsole.log(`receiptSigner = ${receiptSigner}  (tx ${metadataHash})`);\n\nconsole.log(`\nNext:\n  1. Add {\"agentId\": ${agentId}, \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:${base.id}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}\"}\n     to registrations[] in the registration file, re-pin to IPFS -> CID1.\n  2. setAgentURI(${agentId}, \"ipfs://CID1\").\n  3. log\n        : undefined;\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  })\n  .filter(Boolean);\nif (registered.length === 0) throw new Error(\"register() emitted no logs from the registry\");\n\nconst { decodeEventLog } = await import(\"viem\");\nlet agentId: bigint | undefined;\nfor (const log of registered) {\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log!.data, topics: log!.topics });\n    if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;\n  } catch {\n    /* other events from the same contract */\n  }\n}","new_string":"let agentId: bigint | undefined;\nfor (const log of receipt.logs) {\n  if (log.address.toLowerCase() !== IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()) continue;\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log.data, topics: log.topics });\n    if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;\n  } catch {\n    /* Transfer / MetadataSet from the same contract */\n  }\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2657,"cache_read_input_tokens":129540,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2657},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"56cc6375-da41-40bc-b537-8387365257c4","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:40:48.798Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvo8VeZiu9FATaYw7Fd"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Uk7FNHPzPpMWiHGMGLpePE","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/register-agent.ts has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"3050d010-da17-4ac5-8f10-626e8ad2ec70","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:40:48.807Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-08-26T192631Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/register-agent.ts","oldString":"const registered = receipt.logs\n  .map((log) => {\n    try {\n      return publicClient.chain?.id && log.address.toLowerCase() === IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()\n        ? log\n        : undefined;\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  })\n  .filter(Boolean);\nif (registered.length === 0) throw new Error(\"register() emitted no logs from the registry\");\n\nconst { decodeEventLog } = await import(\"viem\");\nlet agentId: bigint | undefined;\nfor (const log of registered) {\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log!.data, topics: log!.topics });\n    if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;\n  } catch {\n    /* other events from the same contract */\n  }\n}","newString":"let agentId: bigint | undefined;\nfor (const log of receipt.logs) {\n  if (log.address.toLowerCase() !== IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()) continue;\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log.data, topics: log.topics });\n    if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;\n  } catch {\n    /* Transfer / MetadataSet from the same contract */\n  }\n}","originalFile":"/**\n * One-time bootstrap: mint the ERC-8004 identity this service is discovered by.\n *\n * Implements design.md §7 steps 3-6. Run once, from an operator EOA holding a\n * little ETH on Base for gas; nothing here needs a human beyond that funding.\n *\n *   OPERATOR_KEY=0x…  PAY_TO_KEY=0x…  AGENT_URI=ipfs://CID0 \\\n *   RECEIPT_SIGNER_ADDRESS=0x…  npx tsx register-agent.ts\n *\n * Prints the assigned agentId. Feed it back into the registration file\n * (`registrations[]`), re-pin, and call setAgentURI with the new CID — the\n * agentId cannot be known before the mint, which is why this is two passes.\n */\n\nimport { createPublicClient, createWalletClient, http, parseAbi, getAddress, encodePacked } from \"viem\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\n\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY = \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" as const;\nconst RPC_URL = process.env.BASE_RPC_URL ?? \"https://mainnet.base.org\";\n\nconst identityAbi = parseAbi([\n  \"function register(string agentURI) returns (uint256 agentId)\",\n  \"function setAgentURI(uint256 agentId, string newURI)\",\n  \"function setAgentWallet(uint256 agentId, address newWallet, uint256 deadline, bytes signature)\",\n  \"function setMetadata(uint256 agentId, string metadataKey, bytes metadataValue)\",\n  \"function getAgentWallet(uint256 agentId) view returns (address)\",\n  \"function tokenURI(uint256 tokenId) view returns (string)\",\n  \"event Registered(uint256 indexed agentId, string agentURI, address indexed owner)\",\n]);\n\nfunction required(name: string): string {\n  const value = process.env[name];\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`missing required env var ${name}`);\n  return value;\n}\n\nconst operator = privateKeyToAccount(required(\"OPERATOR_KEY\") as `0x${string}`);\n/** The payout wallet must sign its own binding; it never needs gas. */\nconst payoutWallet = privateKeyToAccount(required(\"PAY_TO_KEY\") as `0x${string}`);\nconst agentURI = required(\"AGENT_URI\");\nconst receiptSigner = getAddress(required(\"RECEIPT_SIGNER_ADDRESS\"));\n\nconst publicClient = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\nconst wallet = createWalletClient({ account: operator, chain: base, transport: http(RPC_URL) });\n\n// --- 3. Mint. The registry assigns the agentId; we do not choose it. ---------\nconst registerHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"register\",\n  args: [agentURI],\n});\nconst receipt = await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: registerHash });\n\nconst registered = receipt.logs\n  .map((log) => {\n    try {\n      return publicClient.chain?.id && log.address.toLowerCase() === IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()\n        ? log\n        : undefined;\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  })\n  .filter(Boolean);\nif (registered.length === 0) throw new Error(\"register() emitted no logs from the registry\");\n\nconst { decodeEventLog } = await import(\"viem\");\nlet agentId: bigint | undefined;\nfor (const log of registered) {\n  try {\n    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log!.data, topics: log!.topics });\n    if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;\n  } catch {\n    /* other events from the same contract */\n  }\n}\nif (agentId === undefined) throw new Error(\"could not read agentId from Registered event\");\nconsole.log(`agentId = ${agentId}  (tx ${registerHash})`);\n\n// --- 5. Bind the payout wallet ----------------------------------------------\n// register() defaults agentWallet to the operator EOA. Point it at the address\n// that appears as `payTo` in the 402, so clients can verify the two match.\n// EIP-712 domain and typehash read from the deployed implementation; the\n// contract enforces deadline <= now + 5 minutes.\nconst deadline = BigInt(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 240);\nconst walletSignature = await payoutWallet.signTypedData({\n  domain: {\n    name: \"ERC8004IdentityRegistry\",\n    version: \"1\",\n    chainId: base.id,\n    verifyingContract: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  },\n  types: {\n    AgentWalletSet: [\n      { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n      { name: \"newWallet\", type: \"address\" },\n      { name: \"owner\", type: \"address\" },\n      { name: \"deadline\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    ],\n  },\n  primaryType: \"AgentWalletSet\",\n  message: {\n    agentId,\n    newWallet: payoutWallet.address,\n    owner: operator.address,\n    deadline,\n  },\n});\n\nconst walletHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"setAgentWallet\",\n  args: [agentId, payoutWallet.address, deadline, walletSignature],\n});\nawait publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: walletHash });\nconsole.log(`agentWallet = ${payoutWallet.address}  (tx ${walletHash})`);\n\n// --- 6. Announce the receipt signer -----------------------------------------\n// \"agentWallet\" is a reserved metadata key, so this uses its own key.\nconst metadataHash = await wallet.writeContract({\n  address: IDENTITY_REGISTRY,\n  abi: identityAbi,\n  functionName: \"setMetadata\",\n  args: [agentId, \"receiptSigner\", encodePacked([\"address\"], [receiptSigner])],\n});\nawait publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: metadataHash });\nconsole.log(`receiptSigner = ${receiptSigner}  (tx ${metadataHash})`);\n\nconsole.log(`\nNext:\n  1. Add {\"agentId\": ${agentId}, \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:${base.id}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}\"}\n     to registrations[] in the registration file, re-pin to IPFS -> CID1.\n  2. setAgentURI(${agentId}, \"ipfs://CID1\").\n  3. Deploy server.ts with AGENT_ID=${agentId} PAY_TO=${payoutWallet.address}.\n     Its /.well-known/agent-registration.json binds this domain to the agentId.`);\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":53,"oldLines":27,"newStart":53,"newLines":14,"lines":[" });"," const receipt = await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: registerHash });"," ","-const registered = receipt.logs","-  .map((log) => {","-    try {","-      return publicClient.chain?.id && log.address.toLowerCase() === IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()","-        ? log","-        : undefined;","-    } catch {","-      return undefined;","-    }","-  })","-  .filter(Boolean);","-if (registered.length === 0) throw new Error(\"register() emitted no logs from the registry\");","-","-const { decodeEventLog } = await import(\"viem\");"," let agentId: bigint | undefined;","-for (const log of registered) {","+for (const log of receipt.logs) {","+  if (log.address.toLowerCase() !== IDENTITY_REGISTRY.toLowerCase()) continue;","   try {","-    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log!.data, topics: log!.topics });","+    const decoded = decodeEventLog({ abi: identityAbi, data: log.data, topics: log.topics });","     if (decoded.eventName === \"Registered\") agentId = decoded.args.agentId;","   } catch {","-    /* other events from the same contract */","+    /* Transfer / MetadataSet from the same contract */","   }"," }"," if (agentId === undefined) throw new Error(\"could not read agentId from Registered event\");"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{ status: \"settled\", transaction: result.transaction }\n    : { status: \"failed\", error: result.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\" };\n  saveReceipt(receipt);\n});","new_string":"function finalizeReceipt(\n  paymentPayload: { payload: unknown },\n  settlement: Receipt[\"settlement\"],\n): void {\n  const payload = paymentPayload.payload as ExactEvmPayloadV2;\n  if (!isEIP3009Payload(payload)) return;\n  const receiptId = receiptByNonce.get(payload.authorization.nonce);\n  if (!receiptId) return;\n  receiptByNonce.delete(payload.authorization.nonce);\n\n  const receipt = loadReceipt(receiptId);\n  if (!receipt) return;\n  receipt.settlement = settlement;\n  saveReceipt(receipt);\n}\n\nresourceServer.onAfterSettle(async ({ paymentPayload, result }) => {\n  finalizeReceipt(\n    paymentPayload,\n    result.success\n      ? { status: \"settled\", transaction: result.transaction }\n      : { status: \"failed\", error: result.errorReason ?? \"settlement failed\" },\n  );\n});\n\nresourceServer.onSettleFailure(async ({ paymentPayload, error }) => {\n  finalizeReceipt(paymentPayload, { status: \"failed\", error: error.message });\n});"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":848,"cache_read_input_tokens":136142,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":848},"output_tokens":16,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8e6a831a-d229-4aa4-bf9d-7435be37e7ac","uuid":"c7b66970-8062-48ac-ae57-1633879b34b7","timestamp":"2026-08-26T19:41:15.763Z","request_id":"req_011CeRvq8D5xJLwPPHZfgEBK"}
